**[00:01:16.350] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-000003)  
Session suspended.

**[00:20:49.560] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000044)  
Session resumed. Majority.

**[00:20:52.800] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000044-split-2)  
Madam Speaker, Madam Speaker.

**[00:20:56.800] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000044-split-3)  
What's the pleasure of the Honorable Tiangco?

**[00:20:58.600] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000044-split-4)  
Parliamentary inquiry, Madam Speaker.

**[00:21:01.760] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000045)  
What's the parliamentary inquiry of the Honorable Tiangco?

**[00:21:05.120] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000045-split-2)  
Madam Speaker, gusto ko lang po malaman kung bakit nilipat po from September 24 ang budget ng House at naging September 17 po.

**[00:21:15.280] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000045-split-3)  
Your question is bakit nilipat yung—

**[00:21:19.120] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000045-split-4)  
Sa original schedule po na nilabas noong September 15, ang original schedule po ng budget ng House is on September 24. Ang nangyari po niyan, inilipat po yan ng September 17.

**[00:21:33.630] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000046)  
So ang tanong ko po, ano po ang dahilan kung bakit po ito nilipat ng September 17?

**[00:21:37.440] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000046-split-2)  
May we know from the Majority Leader if this is the only agency that the schedule has been changed or it encompasses other agencies as well?

**[00:21:48.390] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-000047)  
Madam Speaker, it wasn't the only agency that was moved.

**[00:21:52.300] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-000048)  
I think there were several other agencies that were moved due to requests from the other departments.

**[00:22:00.000] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000049)  
It was actually the deferment of two agencies.

**[00:22:02.780] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000050)  
No, Madam Speaker, can you please name the agency that was moved?

**[00:22:04.820] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000051)  
Because based on my record, ito lang po ang inusod. Ito lang po ang in-advance na ahensya.

**[00:22:10.280] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000052)  
So kung meron po, pwede pong sabihin kung ano pong ahensya yung inusod ng mas maaga yung schedule, Madam Speaker.

**[00:22:17.020] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000053)  
With the indulgence of the Honorable Toby Chango, can we proceed to the additional reference of business?

**[00:22:22.040] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000054)  
No, Madam Speaker, I do not agree.

**[00:22:24.640] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000055)  
While we are getting the data.

**[00:22:26.480] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000056)  
No, no, no, I do not agree. Let's suspend.

**[00:22:30.000] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000057)  
Session suspended.

**[00:23:00.000] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000058)  
Session resumed.

**[00:23:07.180] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000059)  
Majority Leader.

**[00:23:08.580] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-000060)  
Madam Speaker, upon review of the schedule, the DOT, the PSA, and the Congress of the Philippines budget were all moved to a different schedule.

**[00:23:20.340] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-000061)  
Madam Speaker, the DOT was moved to a later schedule.

**[00:23:22.920] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000062)  
Ang tanong ko, bakit, kasi tignan niyo po, Madam Speaker, September 17 po nun, okay?

**[00:23:30.000] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000063)  
September 17, meron po naman nakaschedule ng September 18.

**[00:23:34.580] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000064)  
Meron nakaschedule ng September 21, 22, 23.

**[00:23:38.680] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000065)  
Bakit naman doon tayo sa dulo kukuha, Madam Speaker?

**[00:23:41.620] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000066)  
Traditionally, matagal na kayo dito, pag nag-uusod, ang kinukuha yung pinakmalapit.

**[00:23:46.560] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000067)  
For what reason?

**[00:23:47.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000068)  
Para makapaghanda po yung hindi lang yung departamento, kung hindi pa rin yung mga congressmen.

**[00:23:52.620] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000069)  
Kasi bakit naman po maghahanda yung congressman na mag-deliberate sa isang budget na tatalakayin pa sa September 24?

**[00:24:00.000] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000070)  
Siyempre, tatalakang pag-aaralan niyo po yung September 17, September 18. Una po yan.

**[00:24:05.960] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000071)  
With the indulgence of the Honorable Tavi Chanko, if you may,

**[00:24:10.140] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000072)  
this schedule, as arranged by the Committee on Appropriations, is not deliberated in the plenary.

**[00:24:15.980] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000073)

**[00:24:18.820] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000074)

**[01:39:59.700] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000524)  
Madam Speaker, I had a pending parliamentary inquiry, so I would like to request the Majority Leader to reply to the parliamentary inquiry.

**[01:40:07.440] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000525)  
May we know the parliamentary inquiry of the Honorable Chanko?

**[01:40:10.060] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000526)  
Ang tinanong ko po kanina is kung bakit po nilipat yung budget ng House of Representatives magpula po September 24 papunta pong September 17.

**[01:40:21.220] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000527)  
As what the Majority Leader earlier responded, the adjustment involved the proposed budget.

**[01:40:27.200] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000528)  
The budget of the Department of Tourism and the Congress of the Philippines.

**[01:40:31.500] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000529)  
Yes, Madam Speaker, alam ko po na hindi natuloy yung sa Department of Tourism.

**[01:40:35.640] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000530)  
Kaya lang po, ang ginagawa po natin, kaya po tayo nagbibigay ng schedules ng budget is para po makapaghanda yung mga members na magtanong.

**[01:40:44.180] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000531)  
So kung ang budget ng House ay September 24 pa, natural po ang paghahandaan muna ay yung mga September 18, September 21.

**[01:40:57.200] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000532)  
So mas logical kung doon po humugot.

**[01:40:59.860] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000533)  
Kaya lang, what was the compelling reason? Ano po yung napakahalaga?

**[01:41:04.200] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000534)  
Bakit inuna yung budget ng House of Representatives when traditionally nasa dulo po yan?

**[01:41:09.800] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000535)  
As I was also the presiding during that week, distinguished colleague, the reason that the budget was adjusted was that two departments were also deferred.

**[01:41:20.580] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000536)  
That is DICT and DEPDEV, specifically the Philippine Statistics Authority.

**[01:41:25.660] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000541)  
Hence, the adjustment of the other budgets to an earlier schedule, citing from the motion of the minority and the majority that there were no more interpellators.

**[01:41:34.940] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000542)  
However, if the Hon. Changko is so much willing, we can inform the Committee on Appropriations that you be informed if in case there are other schedules.

**[01:41:44.060] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000543)  
Madam Speaker, may precedent na po tayo dito.

**[01:41:48.780] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000544)  
Yung budget po ng MMDA, na-terminate po yun.

**[01:41:53.580] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000545)  
Tapos, the Hon. Tino requested that

**[01:41:56.460] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000546)  
It be reopened.

**[01:41:57.960] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000547)  
And pinayagan po siya to reopen.

**[01:42:00.340] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000548)  
So, I'm asking for the same thing.

**[01:42:02.200] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000549)  
Sa budget ng house, na sana po payagan na ma-reopen yung budget ng house.

**[01:42:07.520] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000550)  
For the following reasons.

**[01:42:09.520] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000551)  
Number one, noong pa lang po DBCC, on the first day, nag-request na po ako na dalhin sa committee yung budget ng house.

**[01:42:21.140] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000552)  
Ngunit hindi po pumayag.

**[01:42:23.180] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000553)  
So, sa plenaryo na lang po.

**[01:42:25.660] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000554)  
Hindi po magandang tingin.

**[01:42:26.460] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000555)  
Hindi po magandang tignan na ang budget ng house, eh hindi man lang pinaliwanag dito sa plenaryo at pinasa natin.

**[01:42:33.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000556)  
Kaya po, humihingi po ako ng reconsideration na since pinagbigyan po yung MMDA na ma-reopen,

**[01:42:40.400] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000557)  
eh sana ma-reopen din po yung sa house.

**[01:42:42.900] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000558)  
At tayo ay nasa isang unique na katayuan.

**[01:42:46.140] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000559)  
Tayo po ang ahensya at tayo po ang sangay ng gobyerno na may kapangyarihan sa budget.

**[01:42:51.620] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000560)  
Pero tayo lang rin ang sangay ng gobyerno na may control ng budget.

**[01:42:56.460] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000561)  
Na gumagastos ng sariling budget.

**[01:42:58.760] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000562)  
Yung ibang ahensya po na pupunta dito, pumupunta sila dito sa Kongreso para humingi ng pondo.

**[01:43:04.760] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000563)  
Pero hindi po pareho yung nagbibigay at nagre-release ng pondo.

**[01:43:08.680] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000564)  
With the indulgence of our distinguished colleague,

**[01:43:12.800] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000565)  
we are still in the additional reference of business.

**[01:43:15.560] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000566)  
What you are referring to is under unfinished business.

**[01:43:18.540] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000567)  
We will go to that agenda that is third in our agenda.

**[01:43:21.660] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000568)  
The Secretary General is hereby directed.

**[01:43:23.200] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000569)  
Okay, so babalikan po natin yan after this.

**[01:43:24.820] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000570)  
Yes.

**[01:43:25.500] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000571)  
Okay.

**[01:43:25.800] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000572)  
The Secretary General again is hereby directed to read the title of bills on first reading

**[01:43:30.400] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000573)  
as well as committee reports resolution and for referral to the appropriate committee.

**[01:43:36.540] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000574)  
So directed.

Submitted by the Committee on Youth and Sports Development

**[01:44:17.120] Secretary-General** (asr-000587)  
on House Resolution 1462, House Bill number 11380,

**[01:44:21.420] Secretary-General** (asr-000588)

To the Committee on Rules.

**[01:46:30.000] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000597)  
Madam Speaker, I move that we resume consideration of House Bill No. 10858.

**[01:46:48.440] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000597-split-2)  
There's a motion to resume consideration of House Bill No. 10858 as contained in Committee Report No. 638, and the Secretary-General is hereby directed to read the title of said measure.

**[01:47:02.440] Secretary-General** (asr-000598)  
House Bill No. 10858, an act appropriating funds for the operation of the Government of the Republic of the Philippines from January 1 to December 31, 2027.

**[01:47:19.760] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000598-split-2)  
Majority Leader.

**[01:47:25.960] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000598-split-3)  
Madam Speaker, I move to recognize the representative of the Lone District.

**[01:47:32.840] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000599)  
The Honorable Toby Tiangco is hereby recognized. Please proceed.

**[01:47:37.240] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000599-split-2)  
Madam Speaker, papagpatuloy ko lang po. Inusod po yung schedule ng [unclear] po ay pinayagan po ma-reopen para makapagtanong. Dito po sa House, nagre-request po ako ng reopening. Hindi naman po siguro maganda na wala akong nakapagtanong dito sa budget ng House.

**[01:47:57.600] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000599-split-3)  
Is it a motion?

**[01:47:58.640] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000599-split-4)  
Yes, patapusin lang po.

**[01:48:00.960] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000600)  
Considering po na sa lahat ng sangay ng gobyerno, tayo po ay allowed ng liquidation by certification. So kung allowed po tayo ng liquidation by certification, mas lalong dapat ipaliwanag sa taong bayan papaano po ginagastos yung pera nila dahil hindi naman po natin ito [unclear]. So I'm asking for a motion for reconsideration to reopen the budget of the House and be presented to the plenary in the same way that it was allowed for the MMDA.

**[01:48:30.000] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000601)  
With the indulgence of our distinguished colleague,

**[01:48:33.800] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000602)  
while the proposed budget of the MMDA was indeed reconsidered

**[01:48:39.620] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000603)  
upon the motion of the majority giving way and yielding way

**[01:48:43.660] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000604)  
to the listed members who arrived and who wished their intention to interpolate,

**[01:48:49.180] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000605)  
in the case of the budget of the House of Representatives,

**[01:48:53.160] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000606)  
it does not mean that the leadership doesn't want to discuss the proposed budget.

**[01:48:57.320] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000607)  
In fact,

**[01:48:58.860] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000608)  
the House is very much...

**[01:49:00.000] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000609)  
very much willing to discuss this.

**[01:49:02.360] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000610)  
However,

**[01:49:03.420] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000611)  
if I may,

**[01:49:04.440] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000612)  
and with respectful indulgence to our distinguished colleague,

**[01:49:08.060] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000613)  
Section 105 of our rules states,

**[01:49:11.540] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000614)  
when a measure, report, or motion is approved, adopted, or lost,

**[01:49:15.200] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000615)  
a member who voted with the majority may move for its reconsideration on the same

**[01:49:19.160] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000616)  
or succeeding session day.

**[01:49:21.780] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000617)  
As we have conferred with the Secretary General,

**[01:49:24.660] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000618)  
the Honorable Toby Chanko was on official business,

**[01:49:27.300] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000619)  
on official travel abroad,

**[01:49:30.000] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000620)  
hence, with due courtesy, Honorable Toby Chanko,

**[01:49:34.880] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000621)  
your motion for reconsideration is out of order.

**[01:49:37.640] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000622)  
No, Madam Speaker, it says on the next session day.

**[01:49:41.340] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000623)  
We are not yet on the next session day because we have not yet adjourned.

**[01:49:44.460] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000624)  
So we are still in the same session day, Madam Speaker.

**[01:49:47.800] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000625)  
So you cannot use that rule, Madam Speaker.

**[01:49:50.160] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000626)  
It says on the next session day.

**[01:49:52.760] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000627)  
We are still on the same session day, so it's a continuing session.

**[01:49:55.320] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000628)  
So dapat po kung ganyan ang gusto niyong gamitin na rule, dapat po

**[01:49:59.680] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000629)  
nag-adjourn po noon para next session day, eh Biyernes at hindi na po ako puwedeng mag-motion for reconsideration ngayon.

**[01:50:07.340] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000630)  
I am not actually referring to the next session day.

**[01:50:09.980] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000631)  
I am referring, the presiding officer, referring to a motion for reconsideration of a member

**[01:50:15.620] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000632)  
who voted with the majority insofar as the motion

**[01:50:19.620] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000633)  
to terminate the period of interpolation of the proposed budget of the Congress of the Philippines.

**[01:50:24.860] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000634)  
Within the next session day.

**[01:50:26.960] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000635)  
Hindi pa po tumadaan yung isang session day, Madam Speaker.

**[01:50:29.500] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000636)  
Yes.

**[01:50:29.840] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000637)  
Anyway, just rule on it, Madam Speaker.

**[01:50:32.000] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000638)  
Kung talagang hindi kayo papayag, then just rule on it.

**[01:50:35.000] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000639)  
The parliamentary status is this.

**[01:50:37.500] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000640)  
The proposed, the interpolations of the proposed budget of the House of Representatives

**[01:50:41.820] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000641)  
has been terminated as moved by the minority,

**[01:50:45.240] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000642)  
duly seconded by the majority last Thursday, September...

**[01:50:53.480] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000643)  
September 17, 2026 at approximately...

**[01:50:59.840] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000644)  
before noontime.

**[01:51:01.820] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000645)  
A motion for reconsideration is being put forwarded by the Honorable Chanko.

**[01:51:06.420] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000646)  
While the presiding officer would gladly yield,

**[01:51:10.100] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000647)  
unfortunately, our rule states that only a member who had voted with the majority

**[01:51:14.760] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000648)  
can vote for the reconsideration.

**[01:51:17.380] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000649)  
With that, our apologies.

**[01:51:19.920] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000650)  
The motion for reconsideration is out of order.

**[01:51:22.460] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000651)  
So ruled.

**[01:51:23.600] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000652)  
Yes, Madam Speaker.

**[01:51:24.420] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000653)  
But I think more than the rules, transparency is more important than our rules.

**[01:51:28.960] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000654)  
Kasi kung gusto po natin,

**[01:51:29.840] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000655)  
magigilan ng korupsyon,

**[01:51:32.160] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000656)  
kailangan po may accountability.

**[01:51:33.880] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000657)  
Kung wala pong accountability, kung wala pong transparency.

**[01:51:37.200] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000658)  
So, but if that is your rule, then I will respect it.

**[01:51:42.240] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000659)  
Thank you very much, Honorable Chanko.

**[01:51:44.200] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000660)  
Majority Leader?

Madam Speaker, I move to consider the proposed budget of the Commission on Audit.

There's a motion to

**[01:51:58.540] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000663)

**[01:53:29.820] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-000679)

**[01:53:41.940] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000680)  
The Honorable Vice Chairperson, John Tracy Cagas, of the Lone District of Davao del Sur, is hereby recognized to sponsor the proposed budget of the Commission on Audit.

**[01:53:51.500] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000681)  
Thank you, Madam Speaker.

**[01:53:55.260] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000682)  
I would like also to include or acknowledge the respective

**[01:53:59.820] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000683)  
Regional Directors of COA, from the different regions of our beloved country.

**[01:54:09.740] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000684)  
Madam Speaker, distinguished colleagues, magandang hapon po sa ating lahat.

**[01:54:17.520] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000685)  
It is my honor to sponsor the budget of the Commission on Audit for Fiscal Year 2027,

**[01:54:28.140] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000686)  
amounting to

**[01:54:29.820] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000687)  
16.46 billion pesos, inclusive of the automatic appropriations.

**[01:54:38.040] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000688)  
In terms of new appropriations, which is subject for Congressional approval,

**[01:54:46.540] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000689)  
the total budget of COA amounts to 14.774 billion,

**[01:54:52.460] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000690)  
which represents an increase of about 2.49%.

**[01:54:59.820] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000691)  
or 359.12 million, higher than the Fiscal Year 2026 level of 14.415 billion.

**[01:55:13.220] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000692)  
Madam Speaker, this budget will be used to support the Commission's program, activities, and operations,

**[01:55:22.720] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000693)  
to ensure accountability for public resources,

**[01:55:28.260] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000694)  
promote transparency,

**[01:55:29.840] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000695)  
and help improve government operations

**[01:55:33.540] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000696)  
in partnership with stakeholders for the benefit of the Filipino people.

**[01:55:40.480] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000697)  
The Commission on Audit is the supreme auditing institution of our nation.

**[01:55:47.740] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000698)  
It is the ultimate guardian for the public treasury

**[01:55:51.900] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000699)  
and the bedrock of fiscal accountability.

**[01:55:56.240] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000701)  
COA does not merely look at numbers. It ensures that every single peso contributed by our hardworking taxpayers is spent efficiently, effectively, and entirely for its intended public purpose.

**[01:56:20.640] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000702)  
The proposed budget is a vital investment in good governance.

**[01:56:27.720] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000703)  
By equipping COA with necessary resources, we are strengthening its capability to conduct rigorous audits, expand its workforce of dedicated state auditors,

**[01:56:43.760] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000704)  
and adopt digital innovations that bring greater transparency.

**[01:56:50.640] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000705)  
To starve COA of resources is to weaken our defenses against waste and corruption.

**[01:57:04.420] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000706)  
To support COA is to fortify the integrity of our entire bureaucracy.

**[01:57:11.420] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000707)  
As we steer our nation toward broader economic growth and development,

**[01:57:18.680] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000708)  
let us ensure that our

**[01:57:20.640] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000710)  
oversight institutions remain strong, independent, and fully capable.

**[01:57:29.240] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000711)  
Thank you, Madam Speaker.

**[01:57:31.820] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000712)

at

**[02:05:36.620] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000797)

Kung merong sapat na teknolohiya.

**[02:06:24.880] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000807)

**[02:06:37.240] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-000810)

plantilia items.

Yung ginawa ng COA, arising from that scandal,

Bulacan alone, the epicenter of the, Bulacan alone, mga 50 cases, criminal cases, ang na-submit sa Ombudsman.

Thank you, Madam Speaker.

**[02:12:53.160] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000870)

Mga effort ng COA ngayon na baguhin ang guidelines sa paggamit ng confidential and intelligence funds.

**[03:15:10.080] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001783)  
Mr. Speaker, kailan po ilalabas itong revised guidelines on the use of Confidential and Intelligence Funds?

**[03:15:17.180] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001784)  
At ano po yung mga mayor na pagbabago sa mga guidelines na ito?

**[03:15:30.000] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001785)  
So, sa ngayon po, because it involves a lot of agencies, tinatrabaho na po.

**[03:15:41.760] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001786)  
Ang ginagawa ng ano, ito yung mga observation na dapat baguhin.

**[03:15:47.200] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001787)  
Yung use of aliases or code names.

**[03:15:52.740] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001788)  
Yung tungkol sa evidence of success, yung mga rewards.

**[03:15:58.120] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001789)  
Tungkol sa mga allowed experiences.

**[03:16:00.000] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001790)  
At saka yung hanggang saan ang clearance ng auditor.

**[03:16:07.480] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001791)  
Security clearance ng mga auditor.

**[03:16:11.080] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001792)  
At saka yung paggamit ng mga generic acknowledgement receipts.

**[03:16:16.780] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001793)  
So, ito po ang mga pagbabago na ginagawa ngayon.

**[03:16:21.060] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001794)  
Working progress po ito.

**[03:16:22.640] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001795)  
As to when, I think, within the soonest possible time.

**[03:16:27.820] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001796)  
Hindi pa maibigay ng COA kung kailan matapos.

**[03:16:30.000] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001797)  
But there are now efforts to really revise that 2015 Memorandum Circular.

**[03:16:39.880] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001798)  
Mr. Speaker, bakit hindi po kasama dito yung pagbabawal?

**[03:16:44.800] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001799)  
Ay may kinalaman sa National Security Law Enforcement and Investigation.

**[03:16:50.140] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001800)  
Hindi po ba panahon na para malinaw na may prohibition sa paggamit ng confidential funds,

**[03:16:57.000] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001801)  
ng intelligence funds, ang mga civilian agents?

**[03:17:00.000] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001802)  
Mr. Speaker, it is Congress, tayo po mismo,

**[03:17:10.560] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001803)  
ang naggawa ng mga guidelines through our GAA for what we call the Provisional Conditions.

**[03:17:21.720] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001804)  
Tayo mismo, sino yung dapat bigyan at hindi bigyan.

**[03:17:28.840] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001805)  
So,

**[03:17:30.000] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001806)  
that is yung ginagawa natin.

**[03:17:34.200] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001807)  
Yan ang ginagawa natin.

**[03:17:35.880] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001808)  
Tayo mismo ang nag-identify sinong bigyan at saka hindi bigyan.

**[03:17:42.760] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001809)  
So...

**[03:17:43.360] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001810)  
Mr. Speaker, kung magbibigay po ng rekomendasyon ang COA,

**[03:17:48.380] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001811)  
what would you recommend with regard to the use of confidential and intelligence funds by civilian agencies?

**[03:18:00.000] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001812)  
Ito po yung mga dapat na mangyari.

**[03:18:14.840] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001813)  
Yung observation ng COA, yun na, ang paggamit ng resibo, very generic.

**[03:18:27.280] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001814)  
Yung paggamit ng mga aliases.

**[03:18:30.000] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001815)  
Or code names, no?

**[03:18:32.700] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001816)  
At saka yung rewards, no?

**[03:18:35.160] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001817)  
Ano bang dapat bigyan ng rewards?

**[03:18:37.020] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001818)  
Dahil ba success?

**[03:18:38.620] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001819)  
Ano yung evidence of success, no?

**[03:18:41.400] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001820)  
At saka yung allowed expenses.

**[03:18:43.360] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001821)  
Ano ba yung mga dapat na expenses na allowed, no?

**[03:18:48.120] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001822)  
Or hindi, allowed.

**[03:18:49.940] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001823)  
So, yun po ang mga minamungkahi ng COA, no?

**[03:18:52.940] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001824)  
Regards this Confi and Intelligence Fund.

**[03:18:57.680] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001825)  
Yung IDUS naman,

**[03:19:00.000] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001826)  
ang LGUs, no?

**[03:19:04.380] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001827)  
Meron rito mga intelligence and confidential funds, no?

**[03:19:12.520] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001828)  
So,

**[03:19:14.540] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001829)  
minamungkahi po ng COA, ang DILG,

**[03:19:18.400] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001830)  
na maggawa din sila ng sarili nilang guidelines, no?

**[03:19:22.640] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001831)  
Paano gamitin, no?

**[03:19:24.220] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001832)  
Although, meron joint circular,

**[03:19:26.620] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001833)  
pero,

**[03:19:27.960] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001834)  
so, hopefully, no?

**[03:19:30.000] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001835)  
Hopefully, soon, nalabas na yung bagong revised guidelines.

**[03:19:34.680] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001836)  
Para naman, to answer or respond to the call of the people,

**[03:19:41.040] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001837)  
yung paggamit ng mga intel and confi funds.

**[03:19:45.000] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001838)  
Mr. Speaker, wala bang maibibigay sa atin na espesipiko na petsa, na buwan,

**[03:19:53.260] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001839)  
kung kailan po natin mapagtitibay at mapapalakas ang mga guidelines na ito?

**[03:19:58.960] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001840)  
Mahalaga po ito.

**[03:20:00.000] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001841)  
Dahil ngayon pa lang,

**[03:20:01.420] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001842)  
meron na sa ating mga general provisions

**[03:20:03.600] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001843)  
ang tungkol sa paggamit ng confidential and intelligence funds.

**[03:20:07.020] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001844)  
At nararapat lang na yung mga rekomendasyon sa pagpapalakas nito

**[03:20:12.640] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001845)  
ay mailabas na agad ng COA at ang ibang mga agencies,

**[03:20:17.520] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001846)  
mga departamento,

**[03:20:19.220] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001847)  
na kabilang sa pagpapalakas nito.

**[03:20:26.160] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001848)  
Mr. Speaker,

**[03:20:27.340] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001849)  
as to when,

**[03:20:29.120] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001850)  
kung kailan lalabas ito,

**[03:20:33.240] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001851)  
COA will do its best effort.

**[03:20:37.380] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001852)  
Kung COA lang ang masusunod within the year.

**[03:20:43.100] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001853)  
Pero ang problema,

**[03:20:45.460] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001854)  
meron pang ibang ahensya.

**[03:20:47.920] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001855)  
Enter agencies coordination,

**[03:20:50.700] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001856)  
lalo ng DBM.

**[03:20:53.060] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001857)  
Tagal, daming daanan.

**[03:20:55.540] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001858)  
So,

**[03:20:56.840] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001859)  
siguro remotely,

**[03:20:59.120] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001860)  
sa mga first semester of next year hanggang June of next year,

**[03:21:04.760] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001861)  
may lalabas ng bagong guidelines sa paggamit ng CONFIE and Intel Fund.

**[03:21:13.200] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001862)  
Mr. Speaker, nakita na rin natin sa mga deliberasyon dito sa Kongreso

**[03:21:17.620] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001863)  
kung paano pong napakahirap i-audit nitong confidential and intelligence funds

**[03:21:22.420] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001864)  
dahil nga sa nature nito.

**[03:21:23.880] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001865)  
Na kaya kung pwede pa natin mas ma-restrict,

**[03:21:26.660] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001866)  
no,

**[03:21:27.160] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001867)  
ma-linaw.

**[03:21:28.060] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001868)  
Halimbawa,

**[03:21:28.960] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001869)  
na lang

**[03:21:29.460] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001870)  
ang matagal nang mungkahi na huwag nang payagan ang mga civilian agencies

**[03:21:33.560] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001871)  
na gumamit ng ganitong tipo ng mga pondo.

**[03:21:36.200] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001872)  
Dahil lahat naman ang nasa mandato nila,

**[03:21:38.300] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001873)  
pwede nilang gawin.

**[03:21:39.460] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001874)  
No?

**[03:21:39.960] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001875)  
Nang may transparency,

**[03:21:41.120] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001876)  
may accountability,

**[03:21:42.280] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001877)  
nang hindi gumagamit ng ni-piso ng confidential and intelligence funds.

**[03:21:47.400] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001878)  
Kaya po, sa

**[03:21:49.240] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001879)  
ang tamang panahon dito sa plenaryo,

**[03:21:51.240] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001880)  
tayo po ay mag-

**[03:21:54.560] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001881)  
mananawagan

**[03:21:55.720] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001882)  
ng malakas,

**[03:21:56.600] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001883)  
natanggalin po yung pagpayag,

**[03:21:58.920] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001884)  
sa paggamit ng civilian government agencies ng confidential and intelligence funds

**[03:22:03.720] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001885)  
na nakapaloob mismo sa ating panukala para sa susunod na taon sa Fiscal Year 2027,

**[03:22:10.720] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001886)  
nakalagay po sa ating Seksyon 92,

**[03:22:14.920] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001887)  
nakalagay po dito na pinapayagan gumamit ang civilian government agencies ng confidential funds.

**[03:22:21.420] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001888)  
Naninindigan po ang representasyon na ito na dapat na itong maibasura,

**[03:22:25.420] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001889)  
dapat na itong matanggal sa mga pinapayagan natin.

**[03:22:28.920] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001890)  
Para sa interes

**[03:22:31.120] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001891)  
ng mas mahigpit ng mga mekanismo para sa pananagutan.

**[03:22:34.560] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001892)  
Maraming salamat, Mr. Speaker.

**[03:22:36.120] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001893)  
Maraming salamat, Distinguished Sponsor.

**[03:22:38.120] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001894)  
Thank you.

**[03:22:39.120] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-001895)  
Rep. Sarah Jain, salamat.

Kong Elago.

ay

**[03:34:07.560] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002219)  
hindi continuous yung kanyang

**[03:34:09.420] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002220)  
sick leave.

**[03:34:11.160] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002221)  
Kung hindi periodically

**[03:34:12.260] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002222)  
renewed

**[03:34:17.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002223)  
or applied for, parang

**[03:34:19.320] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002224)  
ganun. Tama po ba?

**[03:34:21.080] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-002225)  
Yes po.

**[03:34:21.730] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002225-split-2)  
Cumulatively,

**[03:34:24.040] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002226)  
pwede po bang masabi

**[03:34:25.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002227)  
ng commission,

**[03:34:27.420] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002228)  
through the sponsor,

**[03:34:29.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002229)  
gaano katagal na pong naka-sick leave

**[03:34:31.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002230)  
mula, kahit, mula,

**[03:34:33.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002231)  
mula, mula, simula. Kailan siya

**[03:34:35.320] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002232)  
nagsimulang mag-sick leave?

**[03:34:38.000] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002233)  
Hanggang

**[03:34:38.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002234)  
sa kasalukuyang panahon.

**[03:34:44.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002235)  
For the record po.

**[03:35:27.420] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-002266)  
Mr. Speaker, iyong katanungan po kung cumulatively, ang nangyari po ay buwan-buwan, every month, nag-apply siya ng tatlong araw na leave.

**[03:35:44.600] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-002267)  
So, saka balik na naman, trabaho. To be exact, magbigay na lang po kami ng data. Ilan na talaga, ilang buwan na yung ano niya, since 2025 up to the present.

**[03:36:05.620] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002268)  
Okay, please. So, in writing po, kung pwede, para lang malaman natin. So, hindi naman pala continuous ito.

**[03:36:13.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002269)  
And just to clarify, yung mga commissioner po ba ng COA, are they covered by the same guidelines on sick leave as other employees of government?

**[03:36:26.320] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002270)  
Di ba, may mga limitasyon lang yan, di ba? Hindi only ang sick leave, tama po ba? Okay. So, hindi po.

**[03:36:35.160] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-002271)  
Cover lang po lahat, pareha lang po ang…

**[03:36:37.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002272)  
Pareho lang sa mga empleyado, therefore, may hangganan din po, no?

**[03:36:41.540] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-002273)  
Yes.

**[03:36:42.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002274)  
Okay.

**[03:36:43.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002275)  
Sige, intayin na lang po natin. Pero ito na po ang tanong. Ibig po sabihin, panapanahon lang. So, tuloy pa rin po, for the most part, the COA can say na nagagampanan pa rin ni Commissioner Lipana ang kanyang tungkulin.

**[03:37:03.520] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002276)  
Is he still able to discharge his duties as COA Commissioner despite his health condition?

**[03:37:13.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002277)  
That requires him to avail of sick leave periodically, every month, apparently. Nagagampanan pa rin po ba niya, ang kanyang mga tungkulin bilang Commissioner?

**[03:37:27.720] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-002278)  
Nagagampanan po naman ni Commissioner Lipana yung trabaho niya.

**[03:37:31.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002279)  
Nagagampanan po. So, in other words, the Commission en banc, yung tatlong miyembro, ay fully functioning.

**[03:37:40.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002280)  
Ibig sabihin, kung nangangailangan ng…

**[03:37:43.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002281)  
Decision, no? Merong mga resolution o merong mga kaso. Halimbawa, itong Notice of Disallowance, ay available po yung tatlong Commissioner at yung tatlong nagpapasya. Ganun pa rin po?

**[03:38:00.760] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-002282)  
Kahit po wala si Commissioner Lipana, ang Commission itself, nagagampanan namin nila yung remaining dalawang Commissioner, they are still doing their… they can do their job.

**[03:38:13.340] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-002283)  
Even without Commissioner Lipana.

**[03:38:14.880] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002284)  
Okay, they can do their jobs. I have no doubt, they can do their jobs. Pero ang tanong ko lang po, hindi po ba may mga pagkakataon na kailangan yung buong Commission? O sapat na yung dalawa?

**[03:38:26.800] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002285)  
Paano halimbawa, kung merong opinion na magkaiba yung dalawa, how will that be settled? Wala bang ganun?

**[03:38:37.300] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-002286)  
So far naman po, sapat na po yung…

**[03:38:43.340] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-002287)  
Dalawa. So far, no? Wala naman silang mga pagtunggali between their opinions.

**[03:38:49.640] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002288)  
Okay, so far wala. And para malinawan lang po, pwede po ba mag-issue ng mga resolution, decision, and so on, ang en banc na dalawa lang sa tatlo ang nakapirma? Pwede na yun?

**[03:39:04.380] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-002289)  
Pwede po.

**[03:39:05.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002290)  
Pwede? Dahil ang requirement, basta majority, ganun po ba?

**[03:39:10.260] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-002291)  
Majority po.

**[03:39:11.040] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002292)  
Ah, okay. Alright.

**[03:39:13.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002293)  
So, ngayon, kung sakaling umabot po sa, sabi nyo, merong notice of disallowance involving the wife of Commissioner Lipana na nasa lower level pa, no?

**[03:39:28.140] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-002294)  
Cluster 7 po. Cluster 7.

**[03:39:30.120] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002295)  
Cluster 7. Eventually, tama po, aabot yan sa en banc. Tama po ba?

**[03:39:36.260] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002296)  
Ano po mangyayari?

**[03:39:38.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002297)  
Will the Commissioner Lipana be involved in…

**[03:39:43.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002298)  
Be decision-making on that resolution?

**[03:39:49.020] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-002299)  
Kung aabot sa Commission en banc level, mag-inhibit si Commissioner Lipana.

**[03:40:01.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002300)  
Okay. Okay, Mr. Speaker, yun po ang pangunahing concern natin.

**[03:40:10.900] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002301)  
Marami pong mga…

**[03:40:13.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002302)  
Nakatutok sa Commission on Appointments ngayon at ang pagtingin nga ay may cloud.

**[03:40:22.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002303)  
Merong question sa credibility ng Commission dahil ang isa sa kanilang, isa sa tatlong Commissioner ay, yun nga, asawa ng isang contractor na sabi na rin ng COA na issuean pa ng notice of disallowance

**[03:40:40.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002304)  
kaugnay sa flood control or DPWH contracts, no?

**[03:40:46.420] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002305)  
So, kaya po, sinikap nating linawin ano ba talaga ang status nitong si Commissioner Lipana at ngayon malinaw na nagpapatuloy pa rin siya sa kanyang panunungkulan panapanahong nag-a-avail ng sick leave at, well,

**[03:41:10.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002306)  
merong pahayag dito na kung sakaling may conflict of interest, magkakaroon ng conflict of interest, mag-inhibit ang Commissioner.

**[03:41:19.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002307)  
Hanggang doon na lang po siguro muna, Mr. Speaker. Maraming salamat po, ginuong sponsor.

**[03:41:25.620] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-002308)  
Thank you, Representative. Salamat po.

**[03:41:29.980] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-002309)  
Thank you very much. Majority Leader.

**[03:41:34.700] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-002311)  
Mr. Speaker, I move that we recognize the Honorable Percy Zendanya of the Akbayan Partylist to

**[03:41:40.380] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-002312)

The Honorable Zendanya is recognized. Please proceed.

**[03:41:46.200] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-002314)  
Thank you very much, Mr. Speaker, and good afternoon to the COA family.

**[03:41:50.980] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-002315)  
I will not be propounding questions as I have exhaustively discussed the points that I need to share regarding the budget of the Commission on Audit.

**[03:41:57.940] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-002316)  
I'd just like to reiterate my extreme concern on the lowering of the budget on capital outlay for the COA as this is a critical part in their efforts to digitize and to

**[03:42:10.380] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-002318)  
computerize the systems within this critical government agency. And I hope that at a certain point, we'll be able to restore the necessary capital outlay for the COA.

**[03:42:21.380] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-002319)  
There being no member of the minority that wishes to interpolate the Commission on Audit's budget, I move to terminate consideration of the same.

**[03:42:29.840] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-002320)  
I so move, Mr. Speaker.

**[03:42:31.380] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-002321)  
Thank you very much. Majority Leader.

**[03:42:34.520] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-002322)  
Thank you.

**[03:42:35.860] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-002324)  
Mr. Speaker, on the part of the majority, we join the minority in the motion to terminate the period

**[03:42:40.380] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-002325)  
of interpellations on the proposed budget of the Commission on Audit.

**[03:42:44.060] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-002326)  
There is a joint motion to terminate the interpolations on the proposed budget of the Commission on Audit. Is there any objections?

**[03:42:51.560] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-002327)  
The Chair hears none. The interpolations on the proposed budget on the Commission on Audit is terminated.

**[03:42:59.740] Rep. John Tracy F. Cagas** (asr-002328)  
Maraming salamat po, Mr. Speaker.

**[03:43:02.120] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-002329)  
Thank you very much.

**[03:46:03.560] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-002378-split-2)  
Representative Mike

**[03:46:04.380] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-002379)  
Tan is recognized. Please proceed.

**[03:46:07.300] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002380)  
Thank you,

**[03:46:08.400] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002381)  
Mr. Speaker.

**[03:46:09.980] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002382)  
Magandang tanghali na po sa ating lahat.

**[03:46:12.500] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002383)  
Distinguished colleagues, it is my privilege

**[03:46:14.500] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002384)  
to sponsor the proposed fiscal

**[03:46:16.300] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002385)  
year 2027 budget

**[03:46:18.360] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002386)  
of the Commission on Elections.

**[03:46:20.720] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002387)  
A constitutional commission entrusted

**[03:46:22.540] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002388)  
with the vital responsibility

**[03:46:24.140] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002389)  
of safeguarding

**[03:46:26.540] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002390)  
the integrity of our electoral process.

**[03:46:28.440] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002391)  
And ensuring the orderly,

**[03:46:30.640] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002392)  
credible, and efficient conduct

**[03:46:32.320] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002393)  
of elections. For fiscal

**[03:46:34.380] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002394)  
year 2027, the Commission is proposing

**[03:46:36.680] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002395)  
a total budget of

**[03:46:37.900] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002396)  
29.5 billion pesos,

**[03:46:40.720] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002397)  
inclusive of automatic

**[03:46:42.720] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002398)  
appropriations.

**[03:46:44.380] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002399)  
It represents an increase of

**[03:46:45.860] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002400)  
9.31 billion pesos,

**[03:46:48.440] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002401)  
or 46 percent, from the

**[03:46:50.540] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002402)  
fiscal year 2026

**[03:46:52.280] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002403)  
GAA of 20.2

**[03:46:54.440] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002404)  
billion pesos.

**[03:46:55.660] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002405)  
This increase reflects

**[03:46:57.280] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002406)  
the need to strengthen,

**[03:46:58.440] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002407)  
the Commission's operational capacity

**[03:47:00.360] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002408)  
as it undertakes

**[03:47:01.980] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002409)  
preparations for the upcoming

**[03:47:04.100] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002410)  
2028 national and local elections.

**[03:47:07.240] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002411)  
Of the proposed

**[03:47:08.400] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002412)  
allocation, 23.92 billion pesos

**[03:47:10.680] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002413)  
is earmarked for preparatory

**[03:47:12.240] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002414)  
national and local elections activities,

**[03:47:15.220] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002415)  
allowing Comelec to

**[03:47:16.320] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002416)  
undertake procurement, systems preparation,

**[03:47:19.060] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002417)  
voter-related activities,

**[03:47:20.520] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002418)  
and other essential requirements

**[03:47:22.520] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002419)  
well ahead of the 2028 elections.

**[03:47:24.900] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002420)  
As we deliberate on this

**[03:47:26.480] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002421)  
budget, we must ensure that these resources,

**[03:47:28.440] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002422)  
are utilized efficiently,

**[03:47:30.440] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002423)  
transparently, and in accordance

**[03:47:32.440] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002424)  
with the Commission's constitutional

**[03:47:34.440] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002425)  
mandate and applicable laws.

**[03:47:36.440] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002426)  
With this, I respectfully

**[03:47:38.440] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002427)  
present the proposed

**[03:47:40.440] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002428)  
fiscal year 2027 budget

**[03:47:42.440] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002429)  
of the Commission on Elections

**[03:47:44.440] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002430)  
for the consideration of this honorable

**[03:47:46.440] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002431)  
Chamber. Thank you, Mr.

**[03:47:48.440] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002432)  
Speaker. This representation is

**[04:07:02.020] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002823)  
But it was not discussed in the meeting.

**[04:07:05.860] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002824)  
It was not discussed in the meeting of the Comelec Advisory Council whether... Kasi po, napaka-critical nung reuse because it can save 8 to 10 billion pesos. Bakit hindi ito napag-usapan sa Comelec Advisory Council?

**[04:07:21.360] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002825)  
Well, Mr. Speaker, more importantly, hindi po kasi parte ang Comelec ng Comelec Advisory Council. So, kaya hindi po siya napag-usap.

**[04:07:29.980] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002826)  
Ay, wala pong say ang ating komisyon.

**[04:07:32.900] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002827)  
kung ano po yung

**[04:07:33.980] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002828)  
rekomendasyon po ng ating CAC.

**[04:07:37.600] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002829)  
Yung pagtatali po sa brand new hindi naman po nila minamandato.

**[04:07:46.160] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002830)  
Pero, dahil po medyo rapid po yung changing technologies po ngayon, yun po yung naiisip o nai-envision po ng ating komisyon na gamitin para po mas sigurado dahil nga po may mga certain issues po ng overheating na na...

**[04:08:03.980] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002831)  
...experience po ng ating 2025 election.

**[04:08:07.280] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002832)  
Ibig pong sabihin, sa napakahalagang issue ng reuse, ay hindi po pinag-usapan ng Comelec Advisory Council ito?

**[04:08:20.500] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002833)  
Hindi po napag-usapan ng ating CAC ang ating kanyang rekomendasyon po, Mr. Speaker, kasi hindi po talaga parte ang Comelec ng CAC.

**[04:08:33.980] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002834)  
So, ibig pong sabihin, binibigyan ng CAC ang Comelec ng Blanket Authority dating po sa issue ng reuse. Tama po ba yan?

**[04:08:53.860] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002835)  
So, Mr. Speaker, hindi naman po Blanket Authority. Ang ating komisyon ay lagi pong kinukonsidera ang rekomendasyon po ng ating...

**[04:09:03.980] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002836)  
...Comelec Advisory Council at ang iba pong partners.

**[04:09:08.800] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002837)  
Sabalit, ito po ay isa sa pinakamahalagang technical issue, whether reuse is possible.

**[04:09:16.340] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002838)  
Bakit hindi pinag-usapan?

**[04:09:19.040] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002839)  
Ito po ba ay, ano, kailangan pang ang inyong lingkod ang magpaalam sa Comelec Advisory Council para hihingin ang kanilang opinion pagdating po sa issue ng reuse?

**[04:09:33.980] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002840)  
So, hindi po silang pwedeng, ang ating komisyon, hindi po pong pwedeng mga alam kung ano po yung napag-uusapan doon.

**[04:09:48.140] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002841)  
Pero yung ating pong question on reuse, open naman po ang ating pong komisyon, pag-aaralan po nila.

**[04:09:55.260] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002842)  
Hayaan niyo po at tutulungan natin ang Comelec na tanungin ang Comelec Advisory Council.

**[04:10:01.440] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002843)  
Nasa ilalim po ng Republic Act No.

**[04:10:03.980] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002844)  
12009, the New Government Procurement Act, kinakailangan ng value for money, market scoping at lifestyle cost analysis.

**[04:10:12.080] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002845)  
Nagsagawa ba ang Comelec ng cost analysis bago nito iminungkahi ang 23 billion halaga?

**[04:10:21.300] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002846)  
Yes po, Mr. Speaker, nagkasagawa po ng market scoping ating pong Comelec.

**[04:10:26.940] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002847)  
At pwede niyo po bang i-discuss ito ngayon kung ano yung mga findings?

**[04:10:33.980] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002848)  
Please, on how did you arrive with 23 billion?

**[04:10:43.300] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002849)  
Ang base po sa kanilang market scoping, ang presyo po na kailangan niya ay 25.6 billion.

**[04:10:56.500] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002850)  
Lease pa lang po ito.

**[04:10:58.780] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002851)  
Pardon?

**[04:10:59.460] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002852)  
Lease pa lang po, contract of lease, renta po.

**[04:11:03.980] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002853)  
Mr. Speaker.

**[04:11:05.200] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002854)  
Meaning, magkano po per unit?

**[04:11:17.580] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002855)  
Ang estimate price po niya, Mr. Speaker, distinguished colleague, mga 180,000 per machine po.

**[04:11:28.040] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002856)  
Nagsagawa po ba ang Comelec ng consultation regarding sa mga posibilidad?

**[04:11:33.980] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002857)  
Halaga ng reuse, halaga ng reuse ng 2025 machines, halaga ng upgrading, halaga ng pag-lease lamang ng karagdagang machines,

**[04:11:42.820] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002858)  
halaga ng isang completely new system at expected useful life at residual value ng bawat opso.

**[04:11:53.580] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002859)  
Mr. Speaker, distinguished colleague, sa ngayon wala pa po na nagagawa on the study regarding sa reuse po.

**[04:12:00.800] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002860)  
So, habang pina-finalize pa po yung ating TOR, wala pa pong final say ang ating pong Comelec regarding that matter.

**[04:12:12.020] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002861)  
And, iniintay rin pong matapos rin po yung final counting nung sa Bangsamoro.

**[04:12:19.600] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002862)  
Nagtataka lang po ako, Mr. Speaker, na we arrive at 23 billion without doing all these matters.

**[04:12:26.760] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002863)  
Bakit hindi mo natin ginawa ito?

**[04:12:28.380] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002864)  
So, upang na...

**[04:12:30.800] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002865)  
Pagkaharap sa budget, dito po, pag-ingin ng budget sa ating bayan, alam natin kung ano yung cost effective.

**[04:12:40.780] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002866)  
Ngayon po, hindi natin alam kasi.

**[04:12:42.660] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002867)  
Hindi natin alam kung gagamitin natin yung dating machine kung kano ba ang magagasos natin.

**[04:12:49.600] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002868)  
Gaya nga ng sinabi ko kanina, logically, mas mura yung magamit na natin.

**[04:12:59.800] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002869)  
Kung po pwede ba?

**[04:13:00.400] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002870)  
Sapagkat nandito na yung mga machines. Wala na yung logistics cost.

**[04:13:08.200] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002871)  
So, ngayon po, kung tatanungin ko kayo, hindi nyo po masasagot kung magkano yung repurbish cost at upgrading cost nung Miro machine kung ito ang gagamitin natin.

**[04:13:25.780] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002872)  
At least estimated po sa...

**[04:13:27.540] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002873)  
Alam ko naman na pakita sa bidding.

**[04:13:29.680] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002874)  
Saka mo malalaman.

**[04:13:30.400] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002875)  
Alam ko naman kung ano yung cost. Pero yung estimated cost ng reuse compared to a brand new machine.

**[04:13:40.440] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002876)  
Mr. Speaker, as of now po, hindi pa po napafactor in yung presyo ng ating pag-referbish ng mga nagamit na dahil hindi pa nga po final yung TOR.

**[04:13:55.940] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002877)  
Tapos...

**[04:14:00.400] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002879)  
What does it mean that the Commission on Elections is hell-bent on leasing a brand new machine?

**[04:14:07.960] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002880)  
Because they did not factor in existence of these old machines.

**[04:14:15.720] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002881)  
Mr. Speaker, hindi naman po sila hell-bent to lease new machines.

**[04:14:22.420] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002882)  
They will study kung ano po yung pangailangan pa kung magre-reuse po ng ating mga machines na nagamit po ng...

**[04:14:30.400] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002883)  
May 2025 National and Local Elections pati ng BARB.

**[04:14:35.240] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002884)  
Ano po bang particular na requirement ng 2028 ang hindi kayang gawin ng 2025 machines?

**[04:15:00.000] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002886)  
Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Colleague, more on additional machines po, yung kakailanganin din, kailangan po mag-input ng ibang measures para po yung mga issues na experienced po noong 2025 elections, pati nitong BARM, iayos na rin po, ma-incorporate po sa ating mga machines na i-release, if ever, Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Colleague.

**[04:15:27.060] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002887)  
I have several questions here. Hindi ko na po inihiling na sagutin nyo na rin kagad ngayon. Maaari lang po maibigay sa akin ng kasagutan sa lalong magdaling panahon.

**[04:15:37.080] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002888)  
Kaya ba nilang mag-scan ng mas mahabang balot?

**[04:15:40.920] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002889)

**[04:15:48.120] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002890)  
Maaari bang i-upgrade ang memory, modem, batteries, o processors?

**[04:15:51.140] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002891)  
Kung mas marami ang precincts, bakit hindi na lamang mag-lease o kumuha ng karagdagang machines?

**[04:15:57.060] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002892)  
Kaysa halip na palitan ng 110,620 machines.

**[04:16:02.400] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002893)  
Kailangan siguro po ay i-quantify talaga ng COMELEC yung additional workload, tukuyin ang eksaktong hardware limitation bago tayo mag-desisyon.

**[04:16:14.180] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002894)  
Kung walang malinaw na sagot, ang mas complex na presidential election ay slogan lamang, hindi bidding, sapagkat nakita ko po maraming mga violations.

**[04:16:25.980] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002895)  
In fact,

**[04:16:27.060] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002896)  
One of my allegations was gumamit lang yung Miro ng isang kumpanya para maabot yung kanyang contracting capacity.

**[04:16:42.580] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002897)  
At yung kumpanya po na yan ay yung mga nakakulong na ngayon.

**[04:16:46.000] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002898)  
Siguro po, alam nyo.

**[04:16:47.400] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002899)  
So, mahalaga po na maging malinaw in terms of reference natin para hindi masisentro baka mangyari yan isang bidder na naman.

**[04:16:57.060] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002900)  
Baka yung terms of reference ay tailored fit sa isang bidder.

**[04:17:05.840] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002901)  
Siguro po, sa akin naman po, it's more of a caution rather than an accusation.

**[04:17:11.840] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002902)  
Mas mabuti ngayon pa lang, mas malinaw na kaysa dubating doon sa punto na kinakailangan na naman ang inyong lingkod ay mag-file ng kaso sa Korte Suprema.

**[04:17:21.860] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002903)  
Mas mabuti pa ngayon pa lang, maisaayos na.

**[04:17:26.180] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002904)  
Mr. Speaker,

**[04:17:27.060] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002905)  
Tama po yung ating kasamahan po dito sa Kongreso.

**[04:17:32.820] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002906)  
The Commission will take into account lahat po ng suggestion at yung mga hinihingi pong mga kasagutan regarding po sa technical specification po.

**[04:17:42.020] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002907)  
Lalo na po doon sa machines na ginawit po ng last national and local elections, sasabit po ng ating komisyon.

**[04:17:50.680] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002908)  
Finally, Mr. Speaker, distinguished sponsor,

**[04:17:55.640] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002909)  
meron po akong ilang mga kasusunod.

**[04:17:57.060] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002910)  
May mga katanungan na nais ko din masagot ng Commission on Elections upang masiguro natin na magiging mas maayos ang procurement natin for the 2028 elections.

**[04:18:09.740] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002911)  
Unang tanong, siguro po kailangan sagutin ng malinaw, may independent study ba na nagsasabing safe o unsafe yung 2025 machines na ginamit natin?

**[04:18:24.340] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002912)

**[04:18:27.060] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002913)  
technically obsolete

**[04:18:30.400] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002914)  
o hindi pa ang mga ito?

**[04:18:32.940] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002915)  
Natukoy na ba ng Comelec

**[04:18:34.220] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002916)  
ang remaining useful life ng machines?

**[04:18:38.140] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-002917)  
Congressman Erice, your time is up.

**[04:18:40.320] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-002918)

**[04:18:44.080] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-002920)  
Thank you.

**[04:18:47.640] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002922)

**[04:18:50.100] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002923)

**[04:18:52.280] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002924)  
Nakumpleto na ba ang

**[04:18:55.920] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002926)

**[04:18:57.680] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002927)

Kailangan siguro po na muling konsultuhin

Sumasangayon ba

**[04:19:25.380] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002940)

**[04:19:35.000] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002945)

Bangsamoro

**[04:20:27.280] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002971)  
First, during the

**[04:20:29.060] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002972)  
2027 budget briefing for Comelec,

**[04:20:31.880] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002973)

**[04:20:33.420] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002974)  
on voter registration

**[04:20:35.340] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002975)  
to ensure that more Filipinos

**[04:20:37.600] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002976)

**[04:20:39.440] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002977)

**[04:20:41.240] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002978)  
national elections. In particular,

**[04:20:43.640] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002979)  
the Commission has set

**[04:20:45.300] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002980)  
a goal to increase the number

**[04:20:47.420] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002981)  
of registered voters from

**[04:20:49.080] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002982)  
1.2 million to

**[04:20:50.940] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002983)  
2 million before the

**[04:20:53.100] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002984)  
2028 national elections.

**[04:20:55.380] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002985)  
Ngayon, September 2026 na.

**[04:41:03.600] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-003139)

**[04:41:39.480] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-003147)  
We acknowledge the presence of the heads and officials from the Anti-Red Tape Authority, headed by Director General Perez.

**[04:41:49.260] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-003148)
