**[04:41:53.000] Rep. Ernesto "Ernix" Dionisio Jr.** (asr-003149)  
Mr. Speaker, I now move to recognize Representative Isabel L. Sagarbarria of the Second District of Negros Occidental,

**[04:42:02.400] Rep. Ernesto "Ernix" Dionisio Jr.** (asr-003150)  
Vice Chairperson of the Committee on Appropriations, to answer questions on the proposed budget of the Anti-Red Tape Authority.

**[04:42:10.480] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-003151)  
Representative Sagarbarria is recognized. Please proceed.

**[04:42:14.820] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003152)  
Mr. Speaker, distinguished colleagues, it is my honor and privilege to sponsor the proposed budget of the Anti-

**[04:42:39.480] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003153)  
Sorry, of the Anti-Money Laundering Council, of the Anti-Red Tape Authority, under the House Bill number 10858,

**[04:42:55.140] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003154)  
or the Fiscal Year 2027 General Appropriations Bill, amounting to P436,720,000, since its establishment pursuant to Republic Act number 11032,

**[04:43:08.120] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003155)  
or the Ease of Doing Business

**[04:43:10.560] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003157)  
and Efficient Government Service Delivery Act of 2018,

**[04:43:14.560] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003158)  
ARTA has placed a significant role in reducing regulatory burdens,

**[04:43:19.840] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003159)  
streamlining government processes,

**[04:43:22.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003160)  
and encouraging government agencies to adopt more efficient and digitalized systems.

**[04:43:27.900] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003161)  
In just 80 years, ARTA has become a vital institution in our bureaucracy,

**[04:43:33.280] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003162)  
delivering reforms that simplify processes,

**[04:43:36.060] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003163)  
reduce red tape, and strengthen public trust in government.

**[04:43:41.260] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003164)  
The proposed budget of ARTA aims to ensure that the authority continues to function effectively,

**[04:43:48.220] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003165)  
uphold reforms, and deliver on its mandate to make government services more efficient,

**[04:43:53.860] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003166)  
transparent, and citizen-oriented.

**[04:43:56.980] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003167)  
Its proposed initiatives, including the enhancement of the digital platforms,

**[04:44:02.060] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003168)  
the Philippine-based regulation information system,

**[04:44:06.600] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003169)  
electronic complaints management,

**[04:44:08.840] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003170)  
and other digital-based programs are directed toward making government transactions

**[04:44:14.020] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003171)  
more accessible, transparent, and efficient for the Filipino people.

**[04:44:19.760] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003172)  
When we strengthen the ARTA anti-red tape authority,

**[04:44:24.700] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003173)  
we strengthen the government itself.

**[04:44:26.680] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003174)  
To empower ARTA is to empower the entire bureaucracy to serve with speed, fairness, and integrity.

**[04:44:34.540] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003175)  
Let us remember,

**[04:44:36.060] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003176)  
a government that defeats red tape is a government that delivers.

**[04:44:40.300] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003177)  
The government that delivers is a government that earns trust.

**[04:44:44.820] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003178)  
The government that earns trust is a government that truly serves its people.

**[04:44:49.600] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003179)  
In view of the foregoing,

**[04:44:51.640] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003180)  
I respectfully seek the support of this honorable house

**[04:44:54.500] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003181)  
for the proposed fiscal year 2027 budget of the anti-red tape authority.

**[04:45:00.000] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003182)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker. This representation is now ready to answer questions from our colleagues.

**[04:45:07.200] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-003183)  
Thank you very much. Majority Leader?

**[04:45:09.140] Rep. Ernesto "Ernix" Dionisio Jr.** (asr-003184)  
Mr. Speaker, I now move to recognize Representative Iris Marie de Mesa Montes of Party List 4K to interpolate the sponsor on the proposed budget of the anti-red tape.

**[04:45:21.500] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-003185)  
Representative Iris Marie de Mesa Montes is recognized. Please proceed.

**[04:45:25.880] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003186)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair. Magandang hapon po, Distinguished Sponsor and to the ARTA.

**[04:45:32.820] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003187)  
Ilang katanungan lamang po mula sa Kababaihan Kabalikatan para sa Kapakanan at Kaunlaran ng Bayan or 4K.

**[04:45:40.620] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003188)  
Una po, your budget, proposed budget for 2027 of 537 million is slightly higher than the current budget, mga around 14 million. Tama po ba?

**[04:45:54.160] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003189)  
Ang itinaas.

**[04:45:55.880] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003190)  
Based on the current year's budget, budget for 2027 is sufficient to perform your mandate of reducing red tape in government.

**[04:46:10.820] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003191)  
No. No, it is not, no.

**[04:46:15.440] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003192)  
Ano po, what programs will be affected given this decrease in budget specifically?

**[04:46:24.160] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003193)  
What programs will be affected?

**[04:46:25.860] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003194)  
What programs will be affected will not be funded, what processes will be compromised, what activities?

**[04:46:42.800] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003195)  
Okay.

**[04:46:44.080] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003196)  
This reduction runs contrary to ARTA's direction of expanding its reach and strengthening the implementation, Republic of 11032.

**[04:46:52.900] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003197)  
The proposed cut will constrain ARTA's capacity to assist the government.

**[04:46:55.860] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003198)  
The proposed cut will constrain ARTA's capacity to assist and capacitate government agencies in complying with the law directly affecting its mandate to reduce, if not eliminate, red tape in government services.

**[04:47:06.040] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003199)  
It will also delay ongoing systems development intended to speed up complaints handling, improve data gathering, and strengthen database security and management.

**[04:47:16.760] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003200)  
Our regional field offices, we likewise have to scale back orientation and inspection activities because of reduced funding for travel,

**[04:47:25.120] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003201)  
fuel, supplies, training, and other operational requirements.

**[04:47:30.300] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003202)  
Mr. Speaker, these resources needed to translate the law into actual improvements in government services.

**[04:47:37.040] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003203)  
Reducing them will limit ARTA's ability to deliver the reforms expected of it.

**[04:47:42.580] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003204)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker.

**[04:47:45.660] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003205)  
Regarding po sa citizen complaints, how many complaints about red tape did ARTA receive last year for 2025?

**[04:47:53.280] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003206)  
Given the total.

**[04:47:55.120] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003207)  
Ilan po ang na-resolve within the mandated timeline?

**[04:48:01.040] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003208)  
Actually, as of 2026, the total cases received was 12,335.

**[04:48:08.760] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003209)  
And the resolved cases within the turnaround time is 11,208, which is 90.86%.

**[04:48:19.880] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003210)  
Mataas po yung resolution rate.

**[04:48:23.200] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003211)  
Kung saka-sakali,

**[04:48:25.120] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003212)  
ano man po, ano yung mga five agencies that received the most complaints?

**[04:48:29.560] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003213)  
Can you name the top five agencies?

**[04:48:38.700] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003214)  
Okay.

**[04:48:39.260] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003215)  
The ten most complained government-owned and controlled corporations from January 2, August 31, 2026

**[04:48:47.460] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003216)  
are the Home Development Mutual Fund, Pag-IBIG, which is the highest,

**[04:48:52.280] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003217)  
Social Security System, GSIS, PhilHealth, Land Bank of the Philippines, PhilPost,

**[04:49:04.120] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003218)  
National Transmission Corporation, Transco, NFA, Intellectual Property Office,

**[04:49:12.980] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003219)  
Tourism Infrastructure and Enterprise Zone Authority.

**[04:49:17.140] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003220)  
These are the ten most complained government-owned and controlled corporations.

**[04:49:22.280] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003221)  
And then for the national government agencies,

**[04:49:26.900] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003222)  
we have the LTO, BIR, Food and Drug Administration,

**[04:49:33.060] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003223)  
Philippine Statistics Authority, National Telecommunications Commission,

**[04:49:38.840] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003224)  
Department of Education, Land Registration Authority, Register of Deeds,

**[04:49:45.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003225)  
Professional Regulation Commission, PRC, Department of Human Settlement and Urban Development,

**[04:49:51.180] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003226)  
and the Department of Education.

**[04:49:52.280] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003227)  
For local government, we have the government of Quezon City, of Taguig, of Caloocan, of Pasig, Paranaque, Malolos, Manila, San Jose del Monte, Makati, Muntinlupa, and Avao City.

**[04:50:14.640] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003228)  
Those are all, Mr. Chair.

**[04:50:16.000] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003229)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[04:50:17.700] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003230)  
So, lahat po ba, have all agencies already established a public assistance and complaint desk or your ARTA help desk?

**[04:50:30.280] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003231)  
And is it also properly staffed to handle walk-in complaints, increase in feedback?

**[04:50:38.360] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003232)  
So, lahat na po, ano pong porsyento ng mga opisinang meron ng ARTA help desk?

**[04:50:44.400] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003233)  
Yes, Mr. Speaker.

**[04:50:45.320] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003234)  
So, lahat na po meron?

**[04:50:48.240] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003235)  
Minsan po kasi, Mr. Speaker, I've had this experience just last year.

**[04:50:52.940] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003236)  
In one of the agencies that you mentioned, sa mga nasa top 10, meron po silang ARTA help desk, pero wala pong personnel.

**[04:51:03.480] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003237)  
So, yung personnel po, diba, the agency is mandated, sila po dapat ang magpo-provide ng personnel, tama?

**[04:51:10.280] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003238)  
Yes, the agency, not the ARTA.

**[04:51:11.720] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003239)  
So, how does ARTA check kung totoo yung mga help desk na yun ay properly manned talaga?

**[04:51:17.700] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003240)  
Because they can always say, yung agency na yan or office na yan has established a help desk, but wala din pong tao yung help desk.

**[04:51:33.000] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003241)  
Yeah, I hear your frustration.

**[04:51:36.240] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003242)  
Actually, it's supposed to be under the law, agencies are all required to establish this ACD.

**[04:51:45.600] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003243)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[04:51:47.700] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003244)  
Yung tanong ko po, Sana, how do you ensure, meron bang routine inspection na ginagawa ang ARTA to ensure that hindi lang help desk kang nandodon?

**[04:51:59.240] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003245)  
Dapat po talaga may tao the whole day to receive complaints?

**[04:52:04.480] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003246)  
Actually, we have a compliance inspection and I'm sorry of what had happened.

**[04:52:14.980] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003247)  
Definitely, in the future, they will be doing that.

**[04:52:17.700] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003248)  
Okay. Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[04:52:20.680] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003249)  
Also, have all agencies established the Committee on Anti-Red Tape or yung CART?

**[04:52:26.580] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003250)  
Lahat din po ba meron ng CART sa lahat ng opisina?

**[04:52:30.400] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003251)  
Ito po yung nag-I understand, yung nag-o-oversee po ng compliance with ARTA rules in each agency.

**[04:52:37.460] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003252)  
And if, actually, if they're not compliant, they will be liable.

**[04:52:40.960] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003253)  
As of the present, the compliance rate is 70%.

**[04:52:49.900] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003254)  
Ah, 70%. So, ano pa pong agency? So, madami-dami pa pong walang CART sa mga agencies nila.

**[04:52:58.720] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003255)  
So, what will we do in order to force them to establish the CART?

**[04:53:05.460] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003256)  
Because it is a mandate under the ease of doing business law.

**[04:53:10.960] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003257)  
They will be issued notices to explain.

**[04:53:22.640] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003258)  
Okay. Thank you, Mr. Chair. Madam Sponsor.

**[04:53:26.180] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003259)  
Meron na rin po ba, lahat din po bang agencies, meron ng Citizens Charter?

**[04:53:33.580] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003260)  
Yes, Mr. Speaker.

**[04:53:35.580] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003261)  
Have we issued...

**[04:53:38.240] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003262)  
98% compliance.

**[04:53:40.960] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003263)  
98%.

**[04:53:42.020] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003264)  
Have we ensured that of this 98% to have complied with the Citizens Charter requirement,

**[04:53:49.900] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003265)  
ay talaga pong sinusunod yung Citizens Charter?

**[04:53:53.280] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003266)  
Kasi pwede rin pong compliance lang.

**[04:53:55.900] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003267)  
So, how can ARTA find out? Do we find out which ones are just complying

**[04:54:01.660] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003268)  
or which ones are following the Citizens Charter to the letter?

**[04:54:05.740] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003269)  
Thank you.

**[04:54:10.960] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003270)  
We require them to submit annual updating of the compliance.

**[04:54:23.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003271)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[04:54:24.200] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003272)  
We conduct inspection also.

**[04:54:25.780] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003273)  
Madam Sponsor, pagdating naman po sa mga investigations and cases,

**[04:54:31.220] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003274)  
how many show cause orders or cases were initiated against agencies or airing officials that violated the ease of doing business law?

**[04:54:46.800] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003276)  
Okay.

**[04:54:48.300] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003277)  
ARTA investigated a total of 600 cases, Mr. Chair.

**[04:54:53.300] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003278)  
May mga naparusahan na po ba dito, na-file-an na ng mga kaso, katulad po ng mga airing officials?

**[04:55:01.800] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003279)  
Nag-translate na po ba yan sa tinatawag nating accountabilities?

**[04:55:06.120] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003280)  
Yes, Mr. Speaker.

**[04:55:07.360] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003281)  
We were able to

**[04:55:08.760] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003282)  
reach out to 373

**[04:55:11.220] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003283)  
total cases filed.

**[04:55:13.640] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003284)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair, Madam Sponsor.

**[04:55:17.460] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003285)  
Kasama po sa mandato, di ba po ng ARTA,

**[04:55:20.300] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003286)  
you can recommend to the agencies, no?

**[04:55:24.040] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003287)  
Pag nakita niyong may mali sa proseso,

**[04:55:26.440] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003288)  
sa sistema, napakatagal,

**[04:55:28.380] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003289)  
so you recommend, may recommendations kayong binibigay.

**[04:55:32.140] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003290)  
Ilan na po ang agencies na nabigyan ninyo ng rekomendasyon

**[04:55:35.980] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003291)  
at ilan ang actual na sumunod po doon sa rekomendasyon ninyo?

**[04:56:03.400] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003292)  
There's a whole of government reengineering systems

**[04:56:05.980] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003293)  
and procedures that are being done.

**[04:56:10.420] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003294)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair, Madam Sponsor.

**[04:56:14.160] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003295)  
Ilan na po, how many procedures were actually simplified ng ARTA

**[04:56:18.820] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003296)  
since its establishment?

**[04:56:35.980] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003297)  
Okay, for the TELCO,

**[04:56:45.240] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003298)  
required permits were reduced from 13 to 8,

**[04:56:50.380] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003299)  
and then documentary requirements reduced from 86 to 35.

**[04:56:55.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003300)  
Processing time shortened from 241 days to 16 days.

**[04:57:01.220] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003301)  
Approved telecommunications permit applications increased from 6,117

**[04:57:05.980] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003302)  
to 10,465 as of June 2026.

**[04:57:13.460] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003303)

**[05:01:04.440] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003360)  
My topic will be the monitoring and evaluation of EBOS implementation.

**[05:01:09.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003361)  
My question, Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor, let us examine ARTA's primary operational line item,

**[05:01:21.320] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003362)  
the monitor and evaluate compliance to RA-11032 activity under the...

**[05:01:28.720] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003363)  
...of doing business and efficient delivery of government services program in fiscal year 2026.

**[05:01:35.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003364)  
This activity received 153 million.

**[05:01:41.120] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003365)  
In 2027 fiscal year, NEP, NEP, it drops to 91 million.

**[05:01:48.040] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003366)  
So there's a difference of 40 percent, no?

**[05:01:51.280] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003367)  
More critically, the activities, MOEs is lost from 77.8 percent,

**[05:01:57.980] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003368)  
from 90 million and to just 20 million in 2027.

**[05:02:05.960] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003369)

**[05:02:18.780] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003370)  
Mr. Speaker, since 2019, ARTA has been actively capacitating LGUs

**[05:02:25.140] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003371)  
to the need to set up and operationalize...

**[05:02:27.980] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003372)

**[05:02:33.360] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003373)

**[05:02:38.720] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003374)  
Mr. Speaker, my question is about 2026 vis-a-vis to 2027 budget.

**[05:02:45.840] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003375)

**[05:02:48.460] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003376)  
So let's concentrate from 2026 to 2027, wag na yung 2019, no?

**[05:02:53.480] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003377)

**[05:02:57.980] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003378)

**[05:02:59.320] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003379)  
Ito ba'y inconsistent ho ba ito sa tinatawag natin to eradicate or eliminate bureaucratic red tape?

**[05:03:10.900] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003380)

**[05:03:14.360] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003381)

**[05:03:22.040] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003382)

**[05:03:27.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003383)

**[05:03:27.980] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003384)

**[05:03:35.120] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003385)  
directly affecting its mandate to reduce, if not eliminate, red tape in government services.

**[05:03:41.020] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003386)

**[05:03:49.940] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003387)  
ganun ba lumalabas dito?

**[05:03:51.880] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003388)  
Because, you know, anti-red tape is supposed to have more budget.

**[05:03:55.560] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003389)  
Now, because of this big cut...

**[05:03:57.980] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003390)  
So, you're telling me now, of course, not you, Distinguished Sponsor, Mr. Speaker,

**[05:04:04.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003391)

**[05:04:09.460] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003392)  
Yes.

**[05:04:09.960] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003393)

**[05:04:10.420] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003394)  
Yes, Mr. Speaker.

**[05:04:11.680] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003395)

**[05:04:18.060] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003396)

**[05:04:27.320] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003397)  
how come...

**[05:04:27.960] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003400)

**[05:04:34.300] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003401)

**[05:04:40.700] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003402)

**[05:05:52.380] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003417)

**[05:12:04.100] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003512)  
including housing, telecommunications,

**[05:12:08.100] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003513)

**[05:12:11.100] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003514)

**[05:12:14.100] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003515)

Mr. Speaker,

**[05:12:38.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003524)  
the Nehemia sectors

**[05:12:40.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003525)  
are at the different stages of implementation.

**[05:12:43.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003526)  
Telecommunication is the most mature

**[05:12:46.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003527)  
and already has implemented streamlining reforms,

**[05:12:49.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003528)  
while the sector-specific one-stop shops

**[05:12:52.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003529)  
for housing,

**[05:12:54.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003530)  
infrastructure,

**[05:12:55.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003531)  
flagship projects,

**[05:12:56.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003532)  
responsible mining,

**[05:12:58.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003533)  
and ship building,

**[05:12:59.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003534)  
and ship repair

**[05:13:00.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003535)  
are at the various stages of rollout or development.

**[05:13:03.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003536)  
For telecommunications,

**[05:13:05.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003537)  
the reforms are already producing measurable results.

**[05:13:09.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003538)  
Under Executive Order No. 32,

**[05:13:12.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003539)  
required permits were reduced from 13 to 8,

**[05:13:16.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003540)  
documentary requirements from 86 to 35,

**[05:13:19.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003541)  
and reported turnaround time

**[05:13:21.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003542)  
from 241 days to 16 days.

**[05:13:24.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003543)  
Approved telecommunications permits

**[05:13:27.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003544)  
were also increased

**[05:13:29.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003545)  
from 6,117 in 2022

**[05:13:32.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003546)  
to 10,465 as of June 2026.

**[05:13:37.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003547)  
For housing, Mr. Chair,

**[05:13:39.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003548)  
the streamlined regulatory framework

**[05:13:42.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003549)  
has already been institutionalized

**[05:13:44.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003550)  
through the Joint Memorandum Circular

**[05:13:47.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003551)  
finalized by 15 national government agencies

**[05:13:50.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003552)  
in June 2026.

**[05:13:52.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003553)  
The next phase is implementation,

**[05:13:55.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003554)  
including the rollout of the

**[05:13:57.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003555)  
Electronic Housing One-Stop Processing Center,

**[05:14:00.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003556)  
or EHOPC,

**[05:14:03.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003557)  
data sharing mechanisms,

**[05:14:05.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003558)  
and the One Map System.

**[05:14:07.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003559)  
For construction permit,

**[05:14:09.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003560)  
ARTA is implementing Executive Order No. 59

**[05:14:13.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003561)  
to streamline permits

**[05:14:14.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003562)  
for infrastructure flagship projects.

**[05:14:17.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003563)  
ARTA is now working with

**[05:14:18.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003564)  
the Office of the Executive Order

**[05:14:20.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003565)  
and the Executive Secretary,

**[05:14:22.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003566)  
DepDev, the ICT,

**[05:14:24.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003567)  
and DOE on the framework,

**[05:14:26.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003568)  
road map,

**[05:14:27.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003569)  
and technical architecture

**[05:14:29.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003570)  
for the IFC one-stop shop.

**[05:14:32.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003571)  
The target is to pilot the OSS

**[05:14:35.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003572)  
for physical connectivity,

**[05:14:37.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003573)  
physical connectivity,

**[05:14:38.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003574)  
IFS in fiscal year 2027,

**[05:14:42.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003575)  
with priority given to projects

**[05:14:44.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003576)  
already approved

**[05:14:45.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003577)  
or outgoing government approval

**[05:14:47.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003578)  
where permitting reforms are expected

**[05:14:49.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003579)  
to have the greatest impact.

**[05:14:51.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003580)  
For responsible mining,

**[05:14:53.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003581)  
ARTA is working with DENR

**[05:14:56.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003582)  
and the concerned regulatory offices

**[05:14:58.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003583)  
to streamline the initial stage of

**[05:15:00.000] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003584)  
of mining-related processes exploration stage

**[05:15:03.340] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003585)  
and develop the electronic mining one-stop shop or EMOS

**[05:15:07.420] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003586)  
through the issuance of the JNC.

**[05:15:11.660] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003587)  
ADB technical assistance from September 2026 to August 2027

**[05:15:16.240] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003588)  
will complement this work by reviewing and harmonizing the other stages

**[05:15:21.320] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003589)  
of the mining regulatory lifecycle, development and construction,

**[05:15:26.900] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003590)  
utilization, commercial operation, and decommissioning rehabilitation stages.

**[05:15:34.260] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003591)  
The objective for fiscal year 2027 is to advance both the streamlined processes

**[05:15:40.420] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003592)  
and their digital integration through EMOS.

**[05:15:44.920] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003593)  
For logistics, particularly shipbuilding and ship repair,

**[05:15:49.420] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003594)  
ARTA and Marina are finalizing a joint memorandum circular

**[05:15:53.360] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003595)  
targeted for issuance in October 2026.

**[05:15:56.900] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003596)  
The JNC shall provide for the streamlining, digitalization,

**[05:16:02.940] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003597)  
and interoperability of the SBSR license to operate, LTO,

**[05:16:08.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003598)  
process to the simplified and consolidated requirements

**[05:16:11.720] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003599)  
and establishment of a SBSR one-stop shop

**[05:16:15.200] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003600)  
with transition to an electronic OSS to the Marina BEST system.

**[05:16:20.960] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003601)  
It shall likewise support investment, facilitation, and workforce development

**[05:16:26.200] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003604)  
to an SBSR investor information package and coordinated training initiatives

**[05:16:32.920] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003605)  
and strengthen governance and stakeholder coordination with an SBSR technical working group

**[05:16:39.300] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003606)  
chaired by Marina with ARTA as co-chair,

**[05:16:43.360] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003607)  
supported mechanism for monitoring, feedback, and periodic review.

**[05:16:49.180] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003608)  
Therefore, Mr. Chair, the work for fiscal year 2027 is primarily about operationalization.

**[05:16:56.900] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003609)  
Sustaining the already implemented telecommunications reforms, rolling out EHOPC for housing, developing and piloting the IFC one-stop shop,

**[05:17:09.620] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003610)  
advancing EMOS for responsible mining, and implementing shipbuilding and ship repair reforms and MARINA BEST.

**[05:17:18.960] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003611)  
These are also the implementation activities that the additional funding discussed

**[05:17:26.360] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003616)  
earlier is intended to sustain.

**[05:21:02.020] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-003693)  
So, huwag na kayong maghintay na lalapit pa yung mga nagtatayo ng planta sa inyo.

**[05:21:11.740] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-003694)  
Tanongin nyo na, wala ba kayong problema sa EBOS, wala ba kayong problema sa permitting, i-assist nyo na.

**[05:21:18.320] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-003695)  
Kasi kailangan natin yun.

**[05:21:20.120] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-003696)  
Kasi naghahabol tayo, we are inviting investors, nag-put up ng mga businesses.

**[05:21:27.360] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-003697)  
Every investor na magtayo dito, may kaakibat po yan na pangangailangan ng kuryente.

**[05:21:34.120] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-003698)  
Ngayon kung hindi tayo mabilis magtayo ng planta dahil sa permitting process na more than 160 plus na permits bago ka makapagtayo ng planta.

**[05:21:46.120] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-003699)  
So, ma-assist man lang ninyo para mapabilis natin.

**[05:21:50.120] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-003700)  
Hindi na tayo magkaroon ng yellow, red alert, rotating brownouts, then every time mangyari yan, tataas po yung presyo ng kuryente.

**[05:21:59.120] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-003701)  
Automatic po yan, tataas.

**[05:22:01.120] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-003702)  
Pati po kayo dito sa Metro Manila, sa bahay ninyo, lalaki, tataas po yung babayaran ninyo.

**[05:22:06.620] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-003703)  
Kaya, turungan natin ang ating sarili kasi yan yung trabaho ninyo, mapabilis para maraming planta, maraming supply, bababa po yung presyo.

**[05:22:18.360] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-003704)  
Can the ARTA commit on that?

**[05:22:23.360] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003705)  
Yes, Mr. Speaker.

**[05:22:25.360] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-003706)  
So, maraming salamat ARTA, thank you Distinguished Sponsor.

**[05:22:29.360] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-003707)  
Thank you Mr. Speaker.

**[05:22:31.360] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-003708)  
So, Mr. Speaker, there being no other members from the minority who wanted to interpolate the budget of ARTA,

**[05:22:41.360] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-003709)  
I move to terminate the period of interpellation and debate on the budget of ARTA

**[05:22:47.360] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-003710)

**[05:26:13.180] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003800)

A significant portion of the proposed

**[05:26:22.880] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003805)

**[05:26:24.500] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003806)

**[05:26:30.440] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003809)

**[06:04:49.100] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004612)  
Are not getting smaller.

**[06:04:50.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004613)  
AMLA owns latest

**[06:04:52.580] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004614)  
national risk assessment,

**[06:04:54.300] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004615)  
identifies continuing risks involving the AMLA,

**[06:04:56.300] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004616)  
involving fraud, drugs, corruption,

**[06:04:59.820] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004617)  
tax crime, virtual assets, casinos,

**[06:05:03.220] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004618)  
real estate, and other sectors.

**[06:05:05.300] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004619)  
It specifically calls for stronger

**[06:05:08.500] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004620)  
financial investigation, asset tracing,

**[06:05:11.100] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004621)  
data integration, and analytics.

**[06:05:14.140] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004622)  
So the budget needs to reflect that.

**[06:05:17.100] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004623)  
Would you agree, distinguished sponsor?

**[06:05:22.020] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-004624)  
Yes.

**[06:05:23.300] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004625)  
Okay, thank you.

**[06:05:25.260] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004626)  
Now, let's go... Actually...

**[06:05:26.140] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004627)  
to my next question.

**[06:05:27.060] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004628)  
Let me move to something

**[06:05:28.380] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004629)  
that the public can understand more easily.

**[06:05:31.500] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004630)  
Criminal assets, AMLA has been active...

**[06:05:35.260] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004631)  
active in tracing and freezing assets

**[06:05:38.220] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004632)  
in case involving alleged corruption

**[06:05:41.340] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004633)  
in government projects.

**[06:05:42.620] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004634)  
In January 2026,

**[06:05:44.820] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004635)  
AMLA reported that assets frozen

**[06:05:48.020] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004636)  
in connection with alleged anomalous project

**[06:05:50.900] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004637)  
had reached approximately 24.7 billion.

**[06:05:54.860] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004638)  
But freezing an asset,

**[06:05:56.060] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004639)  
is not the same as recovering the asset.

**[06:05:59.540] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004640)  
Am I right?

**[06:06:00.580] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-004641)  
Yes, Mr. Speaker, correct.

**[06:06:02.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004642)  
Of the assets frozen

**[06:06:05.020] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004643)  
in corruption-related cases,

**[06:06:06.700] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004644)  
how much has actually reached final

**[06:06:08.980] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004645)  
forfeiture in favor of the government?

**[06:06:12.900] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-004646)  
Mr. Speaker, most of the corruption-related assets

**[06:06:16.380] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-004647)  
are still at the freezing or preservation stage

**[06:06:19.700] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-004648)  
because the cases were filed only recently.

**[06:06:22.460] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-004649)  
In 2025, 15 of the 30 freeze orders,

**[06:06:25.900] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-004650)  
issued by the Court of Appeals,

**[06:06:27.620] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-004651)  
involve corruption,

**[06:06:28.980] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-004652)  
including 14 of the flood control anomalies,

**[06:06:31.700] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-004653)  
covering 20.26 billion.

**[06:06:36.540] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-004654)  
In the first semester of 2026,

**[06:06:39.180] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-004655)  
18 of the 28 petitions for civil forfeiture

**[06:06:42.580] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-004656)  
filed were corruption cases,

**[06:06:44.780] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-004657)  
covering 15.76 billion in preserved assets.

**[06:06:48.460] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-004658)  
57% of all assets preserved during the period.

**[06:06:52.740] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-004659)  
As of June 30, 2026, five active cases,

**[06:06:54.500] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-004660)  
including the one that was filed,

**[06:06:55.220] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-004661)  
five active freeze orders in corruption cases,

**[06:06:58.300] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-004662)  
over another 1.01 billion.

**[06:07:01.420] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-004663)  
These cases are pending before the RTC

**[06:07:04.700] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-004664)  
and judgment of forfeiture will follow trial.

**[06:07:07.900] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-004665)  
The flood control cases alone accounted for 22 petitions,

**[06:07:12.860] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-004666)  
covering about 16 billion in preserved assets,

**[06:07:16.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-004667)  
as of September 16, 2026.

**[06:07:19.260] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-004668)  
Across all of its cases since 2006,

**[06:07:22.980] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-004669)  
the courts have issued 106 forfeiture cases,

**[06:07:24.940] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-004670)  
forfeiture orders covering 2.17 billion.

**[06:07:29.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-004671)  
Of these, 774 million has been returned to victims,

**[06:07:33.500] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-004672)  
166 million has been turned over to the national government,

**[06:07:37.460] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-004673)  
and 119 million has been transferred

**[06:07:39.860] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-004674)  
to the Office of the Ombudsman,

**[06:07:41.940] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-004675)  
while the balance is still at various stages of execution.

**[06:07:45.780] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-004676)  
Mr. Speaker.

**[06:07:46.900] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004677)  
Thank you, Mrs. Speaker, Madam Sponsor.

**[06:07:51.420] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004678)  
If AMLC cannot provide the percentage,

**[06:07:53.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004679)  
does it presently measure this?

**[06:07:54.900] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004680)  
I think one measure of AMLOC effectiveness

**[06:07:59.220] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004681)  
in the number of criminal networks first identified

**[06:08:02.460] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004682)  
through its own financial intelligence

**[06:08:04.540] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004683)  
instead of just being reactive.

**[06:08:06.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004684)  
Would you agree with that, Madam Sponsor?

**[06:08:11.580] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-004685)  
Yes, Mr. Speaker.

**[06:08:14.060] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004686)  
This is an important distinction, Mr. Speaker, Madam Sponsor.

**[06:08:17.900] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004687)  
If PNP, NBI, PIDEA, and the Ombudsman,

**[06:08:22.180] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004688)  
or another agency already identifies us,

**[06:08:24.900] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004689)  
we recognize that we are a suspect and AMLOC then traces the person's account.

**[06:08:29.220] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004690)  
That is important work.

**[06:08:30.740] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004691)  
We recognize that.

**[06:08:32.060] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004692)  
But AMLOC also received enormous amounts of financial information

**[06:08:37.820] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004693)  
from banks and other covered persons.

**[06:08:41.140] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004694)  
So AMLOC should also be capable of identifying suspicious financial activity

**[06:08:47.660] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004695)  
on their own and investigation based on the information.

**[06:08:52.300] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004696)  
How often does that happen?

**[06:08:54.900] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-004697)  
It happens regularly and increasingly.

**[06:08:58.220] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-004698)  
In the first semester of 2026, 751 of the AMLOCs,

**[06:09:04.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-004699)  
1,165 financial intelligence products were generated on its own initiative,

**[06:09:10.020] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-004700)  
including 300 spontaneous disclosures,

**[06:09:13.420] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-004701)  
cases in which the AMLOC itself identified suspicious activity

**[06:09:18.820] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-004702)  
and referred it to law enforcement or foreign counterparts without a prior request.

**[06:09:24.220] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-004703)  
The limiting factor is capacity.

**[06:09:26.900] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-004704)  
Suspicious transaction reports received rose from 10.9 million in 2023 to 62.9 million in 2025.

**[06:09:37.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-004705)  
In the flood control investigation alone,

**[06:09:40.020] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-004706)  
the AMLOC analyzed 5,148 suspicious transaction reports

**[06:09:44.980] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-004707)  
and 492,346 covered transaction reports

**[06:09:50.580] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-004708)  
to produce 52 financial intelligence products,

**[06:09:54.940] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-004712)  
on 393 persons of interest.

**[06:09:57.900] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-004713)  
Scaling that kind of analysis is what the 2027 budget is for.

**[06:10:05.580] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004714)  
Thank you, Mrs. Speaker, Madam Sponsor.

**[06:10:09.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004715)  
Now, I'm going back to my first question.

**[06:10:12.980] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004716)

**[06:10:15.460] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004717)

**[06:11:16.560] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-004730)  
190 million

**[06:11:19.040] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-004731)  
500

**[06:11:21.560] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004732)  
So, 198 million plus

**[06:11:23.420] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004733)

Again, for my

**[06:11:49.260] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004746)
