**[07:07:47.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005734)  
this is

**[07:07:48.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005735)  
a policy that is also

**[07:07:50.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005736)  
recognized by the Philippines.

**[07:07:52.720] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005737)  
Ganun yun, diba?

**[07:07:53.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005738)  
Okay.

**[07:07:55.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005739)  
However, dito sinabi na rito,

**[07:07:57.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005740)  
any military conflict

**[07:07:59.160] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005741)  
in the Taiwan Strait

**[07:08:00.800] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005742)  
would inevitably affect

**[07:08:02.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005743)  
the Philippines.

**[07:08:03.740] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005744)  
So, yung pagkakasabi po

**[07:08:05.320] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005745)  
ng would inevitably

**[07:08:07.020] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005746)  
affect the Philippines.

**[07:08:09.760] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005747)  
Pwede po bang mag-elaborate

**[07:08:11.740] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005748)  
ang National Security Council

**[07:08:13.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005749)  
dito sa

**[07:08:14.540] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005750)  
inevitability?

**[07:08:17.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005751)  
Kasi,

**[07:08:18.640] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005752)  
ang Pangulo po,

**[07:08:20.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005753)  
presumably,

**[07:08:20.760] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005754)  
batay sa input

**[07:08:21.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005755)  
ng National Security Council

**[07:08:23.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005756)  
on numerous occasions,

**[07:08:24.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005757)  
has stated na,

**[07:08:26.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005758)  
sa ayaw natin,

**[07:08:28.100] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005759)  
sa gusto natin,

**[07:08:30.240] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005760)  
madadamay tayo

**[07:08:32.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005761)  
sa gera

**[07:08:33.420] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005762)  
pag nagkaroon ng gera

**[07:08:35.820] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005763)  
sa pagitan ng US

**[07:08:37.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005764)  
at China

**[07:08:39.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005765)  
kaugnay sa Taiwan.

**[07:08:42.080] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005766)  
So, I'm assuming

**[07:08:43.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005767)  
those statements of the President

**[07:08:46.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005768)  
are based on this

**[07:08:48.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005769)  
input by the

**[07:08:50.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005770)  
National Security Council.

**[07:08:53.080] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005771)  
Ilang beses niya na sinabi yan na

**[07:08:56.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005772)  
parang no choice,

**[07:08:59.080] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005773)  
inevitably,

**[07:09:00.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005774)  
we will be involved

**[07:09:01.760] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005775)  
in a conflict

**[07:09:04.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005776)  
with Taiwan.

**[07:09:07.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005777)  
So, ang kinikwestiyon po natin,

**[07:09:08.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005778)  
yung idea ng

**[07:09:10.440] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005779)  
inevitability.

**[07:09:12.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005780)  
Is it really inevitable?

**[07:09:13.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005781)  
Bakit po nasabi ng

**[07:09:14.800] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005782)  
National Security Council

**[07:09:17.040] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005783)  
na inevitable ito?

**[07:09:19.820] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005784)  
That we would be inevitably

**[07:09:21.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005785)  
drawn into this conflict?

**[07:09:25.480] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005786)  
Yes.

**[07:09:26.600] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005787)  
Mr. Speaker,

**[07:09:27.360] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005788)  
given that Taiwan

**[07:09:28.820] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005789)  
is a hundred kilometers away

**[07:09:30.920] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005790)  
from Philippine territory,

**[07:09:33.300] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005791)  
really the NSC is gauging

**[07:09:35.580] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005792)  
the risks regarding

**[07:09:37.420] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005793)  
the proximity in terms of

**[07:09:39.280] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005794)  
the spillover effects.

**[07:09:40.840] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005795)  
So, not so much

**[07:09:41.740] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005796)  
more of the

**[07:09:44.140] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005797)  
evacuation of OFWs

**[07:09:47.820] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005798)  
and the potential evacuation

**[07:09:49.120] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005799)  
of different nationalities.

**[07:09:51.780] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005800)  
Mr. Speaker,

**[07:09:52.740] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005801)  
so that is the basis

**[07:09:54.420] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005802)  
for the statement.

**[07:09:56.600] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005803)  
By the NSC and the

**[07:09:57.680] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005804)  
National Strategic Plan.

**[07:10:00.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005805)  
Okay. So, ang ibig niyo po sabihin nun,

**[07:10:02.400] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005806)  
mainly, it's

**[07:10:03.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005807)  
inevitable in the sense,

**[07:10:07.880] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005808)  
well, inevitably

**[07:10:08.720] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005809)  
affect the Philippines,

**[07:10:09.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005810)  
would inevitably affect the Philippines

**[07:10:12.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005811)  
given the

**[07:10:14.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005812)  
geographic proximity and

**[07:10:16.620] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005813)  
the OFWs in Taiwan.

**[07:10:20.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005814)  
Parang ganon.

**[07:10:20.760] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005815)  
So, yun ang ibig sabihin dun.

**[07:10:23.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005816)  
Pero,

**[07:10:24.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005817)  
does the NSC also

**[07:10:26.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005818)  
see that

**[07:10:27.900] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005819)  
or is it part of the

**[07:10:30.820] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005820)  
analysis that

**[07:10:31.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005821)  
the Philippines would

**[07:10:34.020] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005822)  
be involved in the

**[07:10:36.880] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005823)  
conflict as

**[07:10:38.720] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005824)  
in effect,

**[07:10:41.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005825)  
a belligerent?

**[07:10:43.080] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005826)  
Ganon.

**[07:10:44.020] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005827)  
Na ma-involve tayo sa gera.

**[07:10:48.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005828)  
Meron bang ganong

**[07:10:48.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005829)  
assessment ang NSC?

**[07:10:56.600] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005830)  
Yes. Mr. Speaker,

**[07:10:58.680] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005831)  
more so the assessment of the NSC

**[07:11:00.700] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005832)  
is more of the risk on the evacuation

**[07:11:02.860] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005833)  
of the OFWs,

**[07:11:05.920] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005834)  
the different nationals

**[07:11:07.240] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005835)  
with regards to also

**[07:11:09.280] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005836)  
our humanitarian efforts

**[07:11:10.580] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005837)  
here in the Philippines.

**[07:11:12.440] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005838)  
So, not so much yung kung

**[07:11:14.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005839)  
ma-involve tayo as a party at war.

**[07:11:18.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005840)  
Parang ganon.

**[07:11:19.000] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005841)  
Involved in arms conflict.

**[07:11:21.460] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005842)  
Not so much, Mr. Speaker.

**[07:11:22.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005843)  
Okay. Now,

**[07:11:24.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005844)  
si Pangulong Marcos,

**[07:11:26.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005845)  
and presumably ang mga

**[07:11:28.460] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005846)  
statement niya may input ng

**[07:11:30.320] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005847)  
NSC on this

**[07:11:32.460] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005848)  
matter, and of course

**[07:11:34.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005849)  
the President is

**[07:11:36.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005850)  
the Chief Architect

**[07:11:38.320] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005851)  
of Foreign Policy, di ba?

**[07:11:40.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005852)  
Siya ang nagtatakda niyan.

**[07:11:42.560] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005853)  
In a recent interview

**[07:11:44.820] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005854)  
with

**[07:11:47.020] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005855)  
FOCAP, with the

**[07:11:48.620] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005856)  
Foreign Correspondents

**[07:11:50.540] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005857)  
Association of the Philippines,

**[07:11:53.000] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005858)  
August ito.

**[07:11:54.160] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005859)  
Yung last August,

**[07:11:56.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005860)  
he made some statement,

**[07:11:58.660] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005861)  
the following statements, no?

**[07:12:01.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005862)  
That the Philippines

**[07:12:02.840] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005863)  
will not allow

**[07:12:05.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005864)  
other countries to use

**[07:12:06.620] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005865)  
its bases in the event of a war

**[07:12:08.820] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005866)  
between China and Taiwan.

**[07:12:11.560] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005867)  
Quote,

**[07:12:12.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005868)  
Philippines has no desire to be a

**[07:12:14.320] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005869)  
belligerent nation in the region.

**[07:12:17.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005870)  
Okay?

**[07:12:20.040] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005871)  
And,

**[07:12:20.760] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005872)  
to quote further,

**[07:12:23.520] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005873)  
no,

**[07:12:24.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005874)  
there will be no attacks on

**[07:12:26.560] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005875)  
anyone from the Philippines.

**[07:12:29.020] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005876)  
We have no interest in that.

**[07:12:31.800] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005877)  
Unquote.

**[07:12:35.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005878)  
All we are concerned

**[07:12:36.800] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005879)  
about is the defense of our territory

**[07:12:38.660] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005880)  
and the maintenance of peace and stability

**[07:12:40.540] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005881)  
in the region.

**[07:12:42.400] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005882)  
That is all that the

**[07:12:44.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005883)  
Philippines is concerned with. That is

**[07:12:46.720] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005884)  
all the Philippines will promote. That is

**[07:12:48.820] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005885)  
all that the Philippines will endorse

**[07:12:50.660] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005886)  
and support.

**[07:12:53.040] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005887)  
So, ayun po. Medyo malinaw po

**[07:12:55.020] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005888)  
yung mga pahayag.

**[07:12:56.560] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005889)  
Na, no attacks

**[07:12:58.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005890)  
from the Philippines.

**[07:13:01.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005891)  
The Philippines will not

**[07:13:02.740] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005892)  
allow any attacks from anyone in the Philippines.

**[07:13:05.520] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005893)  
Of course, ang context nito,

**[07:13:07.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005894)  
kahit nung tinanong, is

**[07:13:08.900] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005895)  
the mutual

**[07:13:10.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005896)  
defense treaty with the US.

**[07:13:13.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005897)  
So, paano kung na-involve ang

**[07:13:14.880] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005898)  
US sa gera

**[07:13:16.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005899)  
laban sa China over Taiwan?

**[07:13:18.760] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005900)  
Bagay na pinag-ahandaan

**[07:13:20.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005901)  
ng US.

**[07:13:22.900] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005902)  
No?

**[07:13:24.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005903)  
As far as,

**[07:13:26.560] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005904)  
the US

**[07:13:27.660] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005905)  
defense policy

**[07:13:30.820] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005906)  
is concerned,

**[07:13:32.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005907)  
meron silang tinatawag na Davidson

**[07:13:34.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005908)  
window after some

**[07:13:35.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005909)  
general

**[07:13:37.880] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005910)  
who claimed

**[07:13:40.880] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005911)  
that

**[07:13:42.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005912)  
2027

**[07:13:45.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005913)  
is the target date

**[07:13:46.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005914)  
of China

**[07:13:48.320] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005915)  
based on Xi Jinping

**[07:13:50.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005916)  
pronouncement

**[07:13:52.260] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005917)  
to be ready for an

**[07:13:54.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005918)  
invasion of Taiwan.

**[07:13:56.560] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005919)  
For the forcible

**[07:13:58.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005920)  
integration of Taiwan

**[07:13:59.640] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005921)  
or unification of Taiwan

**[07:14:02.000] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005922)  
with China. So, meron silang

**[07:14:04.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005923)  
nominal,

**[07:14:06.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005924)  
at least as far as the US

**[07:14:07.880] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005925)  
defense establishment is concerned,

**[07:14:11.020] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005926)  
yun ang framework nila na

**[07:14:12.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005927)  
2027,

**[07:14:13.720] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005928)  
China will be prepared

**[07:14:15.960] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005929)  
and may actually

**[07:14:18.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005930)  
launch an actual

**[07:14:22.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005931)  
invasion of

**[07:14:24.420] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005932)  
China. Meron ganon.

**[07:14:25.760] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005933)  
No?

**[07:14:26.560] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005934)  
So, in that event, ano mangyayari sa Pilipinas?

**[07:14:30.160] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005935)  
Sumasang ayon po ba?

**[07:14:32.880] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005936)  
O tama po ba ang

**[07:14:34.320] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005937)  
or

**[07:14:35.020] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005938)  
klaro po ang sinabi ng Pangulo?

**[07:14:38.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005939)  
Sabi, no

**[07:14:39.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005940)  
no attacks.

**[07:14:42.940] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005941)  
Hindi pwedeng gamitin

**[07:14:44.080] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005942)  
ang

**[07:14:44.720] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005943)  
Pilipinas bilang lunsara

**[07:14:47.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005944)  
ng atake

**[07:14:48.880] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005945)  
sa ibang bansa.

**[07:14:51.620] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005946)  
Okay.

**[07:14:52.260] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005947)  
Is that confirmed by

**[07:14:53.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005948)  
the analysis and standpoint?

**[07:14:56.560] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005949)  
of the National Security

**[07:14:58.460] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005950)  
Council. Ganun nga po ba talaga?

**[07:15:00.000] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005951)  
ang pagtingin natin na hindi maaaring gamitin ang Pilipinas.

**[07:15:04.720] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005952)  
Alam natin maraming mga base na binukas ang Pilipinas sa U.S. under EDCA.

**[07:15:11.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005953)  
Many of them are precisely in the northern Luzon.

**[07:15:17.420] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005954)  
Dahil nga ang interest ng U.S. ay Taiwan.

**[07:15:21.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005955)  
So ang pinakamga bagong binuksan dyan, yung sa Lalo Airport,

**[07:15:24.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005956)  
ano pa ba, sa Isabela, meron din, diba?

**[07:15:32.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005957)  
Of course, yung Fort Magsaysay.

**[07:15:34.760] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005958)  
These are currently, as well as non-EDCA sites, lalo na yung Batanes,

**[07:15:40.840] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005959)  
yung U.S. nag-invest para i-upgrade yung mga port facilities in Batanes,

**[07:15:50.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005960)  
including the northernmost island.

**[07:15:54.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005961)  
Yun yatang mabulis.

**[07:15:56.080] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005962)  
So yung U.S. naghahanda dyan.

**[07:16:01.740] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005963)  
Yung U.S. military, they regularly hold exercises with the Philippines.

**[07:16:07.960] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005964)  
May mga nakabasis silang assets, including Air Force assets na nagpapatrolya.

**[07:16:15.820] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005965)  
From Lalo Airfield to the northern portions of Luzon,

**[07:16:21.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005966)  
including over the Luzon Strait,

**[07:16:24.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005967)  
precisely in preparation for this so-called Taiwan conflict.

**[07:16:31.740] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005968)  
So, ganun nga po ba ang pagtingin ng NSA na hindi maaaring gamitin

**[07:16:41.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005969)  
ang mga basing ito ng U.S. para gawing lunsara ng atake kontra sa China?

**[07:16:51.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005970)  
Tama po ba?

**[07:16:52.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005971)  
Is that consistent with the pronouncement?

**[07:16:54.040] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005972)  
Is that consistent with the pronouncement of the President?

**[07:17:03.780] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005973)  
Yes, the agency agrees with our distinguished colleague.

**[07:17:07.460] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005974)  
Yes.

**[07:17:08.420] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005975)  
O, yun lang. Yun yung sagot.

**[07:17:10.240] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005976)  
Okay, then, question.

**[07:17:12.640] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005977)  
Isa po sa mga well-known and controversial deployments made by the U.S.

**[07:17:20.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005978)  
under the Mutual Defense Treaty,

**[07:17:23.560] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005979)  
EDCA,

**[07:17:24.240] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005980)  
and so on,

**[07:17:25.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005981)  
is the deployment of the Typhoon missile system.

**[07:17:31.560] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005982)  
So, meron pong launcher.

**[07:17:33.720] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005983)  
Yung Typhoon launcher.

**[07:17:34.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005984)  
And in fact,

**[07:17:36.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005985)  
initially,

**[07:17:38.120] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005986)  
it is well known that it was deployed

**[07:17:43.420] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005987)  
at Balawag Airport.

**[07:17:46.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005988)  
Itang kita po doon.

**[07:17:47.740] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005989)  
Nandun yung launcher.

**[07:17:52.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005990)  
So, may complex yan.

**[07:17:53.940] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005991)  
Merong mga, mainly, it's a mobile launcher.

**[07:17:58.620] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005992)  
May malaking track, may mahabang trailer.

**[07:18:01.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005993)  
Tapos nandun yung launcher.

**[07:18:03.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005994)  
And it is also well known that the system is capable of launching the Tomahawk missile,

**[07:18:16.040] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005995)  
which is an offensive weapon.

**[07:18:18.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005996)  
No?

**[07:18:19.420] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005997)  
Precisely, it is, uh?

**[07:18:26.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005999)  
We can call it a long-range weapon, something like 1,600 kilometers

**[07:18:29.440] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006000)  
mula sa Northern Luzon, abot na abot sa, hindi lang sa Taiwan,

**[07:18:33.240] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006001)  
kung hindi kahit sa mainland China.

**[07:18:36.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006002)  
At hindi lang dineploy doon, nananatili pa rin nakadeploy ngayon,

**[07:18:41.460] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006003)  
and just recently, a few months ago, there was a test firing

**[07:18:45.660] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006004)  
of the Typhoon missile system from Tacloban Airport

**[07:18:50.400] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006005)  
tapos yung target, the test target was in Fort Magsaysay in Nueva Ecija,

**[07:18:57.020] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006006)  
over 600 kilometers daw.

**[07:18:59.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006007)  
Okay, so ano ibig sabihin nun?

**[07:19:04.840] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006008)  
Does that not contradict the open pronouncements of the President himself

**[07:19:12.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006009)  
and the NSC now, na hindi pa hihintulutan ng Pilipinas na maging lunsaran,

**[07:19:18.960] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006010)  
eh meron nga offensive missile system ang mga Amerikano dito

**[07:19:28.620] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006011)  
at hindi lang yun, tinesting pa nila.

**[07:19:32.460] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006012)  
At malinaw naman, gagamitin yun, hindi sa loob ng Pilipinas.

**[07:19:37.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006013)  
Kung hindi gagamitin yun, for offensive purposes,

**[07:19:42.460] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006014)  
against, in the end, it will be against China in the Taiwan conflict.

**[07:19:48.100] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006015)  
So, paano po yun?

**[07:19:48.840] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006016)  
Paano po yun?

**[07:19:48.940] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006017)  
How do we reconcile?

**[07:19:50.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006018)  
Sabi ng Pangulo, no offensive weapons, no attacks from the Philippines,

**[07:19:55.400] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006019)  
and yet, nag-host ng offensive missile system,

**[07:20:02.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006020)  
with such capability, the Typhoon missile system, ang US.

**[07:20:06.740] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006021)  
Ano pong paliwanag ng NSC dito?

**[07:20:10.480] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006022)  
Yes, Mr. Speaker, the stance of the NSC is that

**[07:20:15.640] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006023)  
these exercises actually are exercise of,

**[07:20:18.940] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006024)  
of Philippine sovereignty.

**[07:20:21.040] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006025)  
With, particularly, with this launcher,

**[07:20:24.280] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006026)  
this is an assessment of the capabilities,

**[07:20:28.900] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006027)  
also of the Philippines, with regards to its defense posture.

**[07:20:33.280] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006028)  
So, it's not in itself an offensive exercise.

**[07:20:41.320] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006029)  
It's really just to measure and assess the capabilities of the Philippines

**[07:20:46.060] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006030)  
with regards to defending its territory, Mr. Speaker.

**[07:20:48.940] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006031)  
Well, thank you for that.

**[07:20:51.740] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006032)  
Well, I just wanted to point out here,

**[07:20:53.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006033)  
there seems to be a contradiction between the public pronouncements

**[07:20:58.840] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006034)  
of the President himself, no less than the President.

**[07:21:01.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006035)  
Sinasabi niya sa local and international audiences

**[07:21:05.100] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006036)  
na hindi niya papaintulutan na gamitin ang teritoryo ng Pilipinas

**[07:21:10.240] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006037)  
para gawing lunsaran ng atake, lalo na ng US,

**[07:21:15.840] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006038)  
dahil ayaw natin maging party sa conflict.

**[07:21:18.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006039)  
On the other hand, patuloy naman ang pag-host

**[07:21:25.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006040)  
ng, ayun na nga, those very offensive weapon systems

**[07:21:31.540] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006041)  
that only have one use.

**[07:21:35.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006042)  
The only purpose of the US for that is to attack, obviously.

**[07:21:41.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006043)  
It's an adversary in whatever conflict, diba?

**[07:21:48.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006044)  
So, yun po, pinopoint out natin.

**[07:21:51.440] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006045)  
Either pagkakatiwalaan natin ang sinabi ng Pangulo

**[07:21:56.900] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006046)  
o hindi buong katapatan ang naririnig natin

**[07:22:02.400] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006047)  
kaugnay sa patakarang ito.

**[07:22:03.840] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006048)  
Meron pong kontradiksyon.

**[07:22:05.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006049)  
Yun po ang pinopoint out natin.

**[07:22:07.260] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006050)  
Okay, needless to say,

**[07:22:09.740] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006051)  
ang punto rito, kung totoo ang sinasabi ng Pangulo

**[07:22:14.320] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006052)  
at ang patakaran ng NSC,

**[07:22:16.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006053)  
ay dapat i-withdrawal.

**[07:22:18.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006054)  
I-withdraw yung typhoon missile system na yan.

**[07:22:24.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006055)  
Diba?

**[07:22:25.260] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006056)  
Then, malinaw,

**[07:22:26.880] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006057)  
walang offensive intent

**[07:22:33.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006058)  
na pinahihintulutan ang Pilipinas

**[07:22:39.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006059)  
as an exercise of its sovereignty

**[07:22:41.520] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006060)  
and in accordance with the public pronouncement of the President.

**[07:22:45.160] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006061)  
Let me move on.

**[07:22:46.640] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006062)  
Alam natin, limited ang oras.

**[07:22:48.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006063)  
May tanong naman tayo kaugnay sa National Action Plan

**[07:22:52.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006064)  
for Unity, Peace, and Development.

**[07:22:57.120] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006065)  
Yung tinatawag na NAP-UPD.

**[07:23:00.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006066)  
Okay.

**[07:23:04.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006067)  
This has more to do with internal security.

**[07:23:10.540] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006068)  
Kung yung isa, pinag-usapan natin, external.

**[07:23:13.040] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006069)  
Ito, internal.

**[07:23:15.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006070)  
At alam natin, ang lead agency na nagpapatupad,

**[07:23:18.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006071)  
ng tinatawag na NAP-UPD is the NTFL-CAC.

**[07:23:23.940] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006072)  
We also know that the vice chair of the NTFL-CAC

**[07:23:27.820] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006073)  
is the National Security Council.

**[07:23:29.640] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006074)  
Kaya dito po natin tinatanong.

**[07:23:31.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006075)  
Okay.

**[07:23:32.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006076)  
Actually,

**[07:23:34.900] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006077)  
ang isang tanong ko lang po, muna,

**[07:23:37.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006078)  
ay tungkol sa legislative agenda.

**[07:23:39.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006079)  
As found in the document,

**[07:23:41.940] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006080)  
the NAP-UPD document,

**[07:23:44.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006081)  
on page 41 of the said document,

**[07:23:48.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006082)  
nakalagay po dito,

**[07:23:52.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006083)  
as part of the legislative and policy agenda,

**[07:23:56.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006084)  
it says here,

**[07:23:57.440] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006085)  
the NTFL-CAC shall advocate the passage of laws

**[07:24:01.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006086)  
and related policies on the following.

**[07:24:04.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006087)  
There's one, institutionalizing the whole-of-nation approach,

**[07:24:07.520] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006088)  
and then there's number two,

**[07:24:09.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006089)  
increasing the role and responsibilities of LGUs

**[07:24:12.260] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006090)  
and the Philippine National Police

**[07:24:14.440] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006091)  
in addressing internal security,

**[07:24:17.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006092)  
particularly through

**[07:24:19.840] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006094)  
amendment of Republic Act 8551

**[07:24:21.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006095)  
to transfer the lead role and responsibilities

**[07:24:25.080] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006096)  
in internal security operations to PNP

**[07:24:28.120] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006097)  
with the support of the AFP.

**[07:24:31.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006098)  
So, apparently, yun ang dalawang,

**[07:24:33.620] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006099)  
dalawa lang yung legislative agenda

**[07:24:35.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006100)  
under this National Action Plan

**[07:24:39.400] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006101)  
for Unity, Peace, and Development 2025 to 2028.

**[07:24:43.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006102)  
So, it's until the end of the

**[07:24:45.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006103)  
Marcos Jr. administration's term.

**[07:24:48.900] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006104)  
Yung sa pangalawa po,

**[07:24:52.400] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006105)  
parang narinig na natin yan dati, diba?

**[07:24:54.240] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006106)  
And in fact, under the Ramos administration,

**[07:24:57.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006107)  
that was implemented.

**[07:25:00.120] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006108)  
After the repeal of the anti-subversion law,

**[07:25:04.760] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006109)  
and then there were peace process,

**[07:25:07.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006110)  
peace talks,

**[07:25:09.440] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006111)  
ay pinahawak sa PNP yung counter-insurgency.

**[07:25:16.740] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006112)  
So, apparently,

**[07:25:18.900] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006113)  
yan uli ang policy direction

**[07:25:22.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006114)  
ng under this plan.

**[07:25:26.540] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006115)  
Una muna,

**[07:25:28.080] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006116)  
ano po ang basis nun?

**[07:25:29.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006117)  
Bakit may ganitong

**[07:25:30.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006118)  
legislative and policy agenda

**[07:25:34.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006119)  
ang NTF-ELCAC?

**[07:25:37.900] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006120)  
Basis.

**[07:25:39.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006121)  
What is the basis?

**[07:25:39.880] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006122)  
Yes, Mr. Speaker.

**[07:25:51.440] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006123)  
This might be related to

**[07:25:53.620] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006124)  
the comprehensive

**[07:25:54.760] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006125)  
archipelagic defense concept,

**[07:25:57.380] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006126)  
which basically,

**[07:25:59.820] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006127)  
since we're

**[07:26:02.000] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006128)  
refocusing,

**[07:26:04.400] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006129)  
we're shifting

**[07:26:04.980] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006130)  
the defense posture of the Philippines

**[07:26:07.880] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006131)  
towards the

**[07:26:09.860] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006132)  
protection of our

**[07:26:11.240] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006133)  
exclusive economic zones.

**[07:26:14.820] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006134)  
So, given that

**[07:26:16.060] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006135)  
the military would be

**[07:26:17.940] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006136)  
reoriented towards

**[07:26:19.820] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006137)  
the protection of our

**[07:26:21.040] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006138)  
EEZ,

**[07:26:23.300] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006139)  
of course,

**[07:26:24.260] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006140)  
that would create

**[07:26:25.760] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006141)  
a vacuum

**[07:26:28.320] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006142)  
that the PNP,

**[07:26:32.240] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006143)  
the police,

**[07:26:33.180] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006144)  
and the LGUs

**[07:26:35.060] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006145)  
should fill.

**[07:26:37.140] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006146)  
Given the

**[07:26:38.980] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006147)  
perspective.

**[07:26:39.860] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006148)  
Okay, so,

**[07:26:41.840] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006149)  
this is related to the shift

**[07:26:43.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006150)  
in focus of the ANP

**[07:26:45.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006151)  
to external defense.

**[07:26:48.140] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006152)  
Towards the protection

**[07:26:49.840] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006153)  
of our exclusive economic zones.

**[07:26:51.640] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006154)  
External defense, including

**[07:26:52.840] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006155)  
exclusive economic zones.

**[07:26:57.000] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006156)  
Okay.

**[07:26:57.560] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006157)  
Actually, yun naman talaga

**[07:26:58.820] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006158)  
ang pangunahing,

**[07:26:59.760] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006159)  
under the Constitution,

**[07:27:00.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006160)  
yun ang pangunahing

**[07:27:01.940] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006161)  
mandato,

**[07:27:03.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006162)  
diba,

**[07:27:05.000] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006163)  
ng armed forces,

**[07:27:07.420] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006164)  
external defense,

**[07:32:27.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006331)

**[07:32:28.640] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006332)  
So...

**[07:32:30.000] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006333)  
Kung babasahin mo, parang napakabanayad ng mga pangyayari at nakamit ang mga tagumpay.

**[07:32:35.760] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006334)  
Pero sa totoo lang, yung binabangit ng pangyayari dito,

**[07:32:39.440] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006335)  
ito yung ilang taon ng marahas na red tagging, harassment, violation of civil rights and human rights

**[07:32:51.020] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006336)  
of individuals from a very wide spectrum of society mula sa mga profesional, sa mga guro,

**[07:33:02.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006337)  
sa mga kawani, sa gobyerno, sa labas ng gobyerno, media, pati mga artista, mga workers, mga unionists,

**[07:33:13.520] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006338)  
mga indigenous peoples, and so on and so forth.

**[07:33:17.320] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006339)  
Alam natin yan, yung red tagging. Kaya nga, alam na natin.

**[07:33:20.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006340)  
At tumatak talaga yung issue ng red tagging. This is it.

**[07:33:25.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006341)  
Yung sinasabing situational awareness and strategic communication initiatives.

**[07:33:37.820] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006342)  
Ayan po yung constant red tagging on social media using paid trolls and so on and so forth.

**[07:33:48.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006343)  
Using government resources.

**[07:33:50.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006344)  
Yung Supreme Court mismo, naglabas ng ruling, yung kaugnay sa writ of amparo, yung deduro ruling,

**[07:34:04.420] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006345)  
na nagsabi, red tagging is dangerous for the targets of red tagging.

**[07:34:14.620] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006346)  
So, yun po ang point natin dito.

**[07:34:20.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006347)  
Ang paglalahad ng mga pangyayari, makikita mo na lang sa bandang dulo dahil may sinabi doon yung pinasara yung mga salugpungan schools.

**[07:34:29.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006348)  
And that was by no means a peaceful process.

**[07:34:34.560] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006349)  
Kung hindi, madugo po yan. Literally, in some cases, may mga namatay po.

**[07:34:40.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006350)  
Maraming pinalikas, maraming mga na-detain, may mga nag-disappear, and so on and so forth.

**[07:34:48.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006351)  
Okay.

**[07:34:49.900] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006352)  
So,

**[07:34:50.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006353)  
yun po yung unang nire-rehistro natin.

**[07:34:53.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006354)  
And then, to illustrate my point,

**[07:34:56.720] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006355)  
hindi po natatapos to

**[07:34:57.880] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006356)  
dun sa panahon ng nakaraang administrasyon,

**[07:35:00.640] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006357)

**[08:00:20.720] Deputy Speaker Yasser Alonto Balindong** (asr-006816)  
There is a joint motion

**[08:00:22.480] Deputy Speaker Yasser Alonto Balindong** (asr-006817)

**[08:00:23.720] Deputy Speaker Yasser Alonto Balindong** (asr-006818)

**[08:00:26.260] Deputy Speaker Yasser Alonto Balindong** (asr-006819)  
budget of the National Security

**[08:00:28.460] Deputy Speaker Yasser Alonto Balindong** (asr-006820)  
Council.

**[08:00:30.000] Deputy Speaker Yasser Alonto Balindong** (asr-006821)  
Is there any objection?

**[08:00:32.040] Deputy Speaker Yasser Alonto Balindong** (asr-006822)  
Hearing none, the interpellation

**[08:00:34.300] Deputy Speaker Yasser Alonto Balindong** (asr-006823)  
on the proposed budget of the

**[08:00:36.100] Deputy Speaker Yasser Alonto Balindong** (asr-006824)  
National Security Council

**[08:00:38.340] Deputy Speaker Yasser Alonto Balindong** (asr-006825)

**[08:00:41.420] Deputy Speaker Yasser Alonto Balindong** (asr-006826)  
You are the leader.

**[08:00:49.640] Rep. Katrina Reiko Chua-Tai** (asr-006827)  
Mr. Speaker,

**[08:00:50.820] Rep. Katrina Reiko Chua-Tai** (asr-006828)  
I move to suspend for a few minutes.

**[08:03:37.020] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-006837)  
Majority Leader,

**[08:03:38.980] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-006838)

**[08:03:40.800] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-006839)

**[08:03:43.400] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-006840)

**[08:03:45.380] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-006841)

**[08:03:47.680] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-006842)

**[08:03:49.540] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-006843)

**[08:03:51.860] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-006844)

**[08:04:20.580] Rep. Katrina Reiko Chua-Tai** (asr-006848)  
the proposed budget of the

**[08:04:22.780] Rep. Katrina Reiko Chua-Tai** (asr-006849)  
National Amnesty

**[08:04:24.400] Rep. Katrina Reiko Chua-Tai** (asr-006850)  
Commission.

**[08:04:25.160] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-006851)

**[08:04:27.500] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-006852)

**[08:04:29.160] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-006853)

**[08:04:35.180] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-006856)

**[08:04:36.480] Rep. Katrina Reiko Chua-Tai** (asr-006857)

**[08:04:40.420] Rep. Katrina Reiko Chua-Tai** (asr-006859)

**[08:04:47.680] Rep. Katrina Reiko Chua-Tai** (asr-006863)  
Jamar M.

**[08:04:50.500] Rep. Katrina Reiko Chua-Tai** (asr-006864)  
Kulayan, Commissioner

**[08:04:52.180] Rep. Katrina Reiko Chua-Tai** (asr-006865)  
Ser-me L.

**[08:04:54.800] Rep. Katrina Reiko Chua-Tai** (asr-006866)  
Ayuyao.

**[08:04:58.398] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-006867)  
To the officers,

**[08:05:00.638] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-006868)  
and the heads of the National Amnesty Commission,

**[08:05:03.160] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-006869)  
namely Chairperson Leah Tanodra Armamento,

**[08:05:07.400] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-006870)  
Commissioner Jamar M. Kulayan,

**[08:05:10.000] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-006871)  
and Commissioner Ser-me Ayuyao,

**[08:05:12.200] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-006872)  
on behalf of Speaker Bojie Dy

**[08:05:15.120] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-006873)  
and Majority Leader Sandro Marcos,

**[08:05:17.480] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-006874)  
Sir, Ma'am, good evening.

Welcome to the House of Representatives.

**[08:05:21.920] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-006876)  
Majority Leader.

**[08:05:25.200] Rep. Katrina Reiko Chua-Tai** (asr-006877)  
Mr. Speaker, I move that we open the period of sponsorship and debate.

**[08:05:29.840] Rep. Katrina Reiko Chua-Tai** (asr-006879)  
I move that we recognize the Honorable Jose Joboy S. Aquino II,

**[08:05:35.840] Rep. Katrina Reiko Chua-Tai** (asr-006880)  
Vice Chairperson of Committee on Appropriation

**[08:05:40.240] Rep. Katrina Reiko Chua-Tai** (asr-006881)  
to sponsor the proposed budget of the National Amnesty Commission.

**[08:05:45.240] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-006882)  
The gentleman from the Lone District of Butuan, Vice Chair Joboy Aquino,

**[08:05:51.240] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-006883)  
Mayong Gabie, you are recognized.

**[08:05:54.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006884)  
Yes, Mayong Gabie, Mr. Speaker,

**[08:05:57.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006885)  
distinguished colleagues,

**[08:05:59.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006886)  
it is my privilege to sponsor the proposed budget of the National Amnesty Commission

**[08:06:06.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006887)  
for the fiscal year 2027,

**[08:06:09.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006888)  
amounting to 266,700,000.

**[08:06:15.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006889)  
As an institution entrusted with implementing the Government's Amnesty Program

**[08:06:23.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006890)  
for qualified beneficiaries in accordance with existing laws

**[08:06:28.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006891)  
and proclamations,

**[08:06:30.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006892)  
the Commission plays an important role in supporting the Government's efforts

**[08:06:36.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006893)  
towards reconciliation, reintegration, and the promotion of lasting peace

**[08:06:43.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006894)  
by providing a lawful mechanism through which qualified individuals

**[08:06:50.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006895)  
may avail themselves of amnesty.

**[08:06:54.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006896)  
Its work requires

**[08:06:57.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006897)  
not only the proper evaluation and processing of applicants,

**[08:07:05.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006898)  
but also effective coordination with concerned government agencies

**[08:07:11.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006899)  
to ensure that the objectives of the Amnesty Program

**[08:07:16.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006900)  
are carried out efficiently and in accordance with the law.

**[08:07:21.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006901)  
As we deliberate on the fiscal year 2027,

**[08:07:25.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006902)  
budget of the National Amnesty Commission,

**[08:07:30.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006903)  
we recognize the importance of providing the resources necessary

**[08:07:36.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006904)  
for the Commission to fulfill its mandate

**[08:07:40.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006905)  
while ensuring accountability and prudent use of public funds.

**[08:07:47.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006906)  
The Commission's appropriations must support the effective administration

**[08:07:53.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006907)  
of the Amnesty Program,

**[08:07:56.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006908)  
including the processing and issuance of amnesty grants

**[08:08:01.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006909)  
as well as the institutional requirements necessary for its operations.

**[08:08:08.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006910)  
In view of the foregoing,

**[08:08:10.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006911)  
I therefore ask our fellow members of this August Chamber

**[08:08:14.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006912)  
to support the proposed budget of the National Amnesty Commission

**[08:08:20.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006913)  
for the successful implementation

**[08:08:22.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006914)  
of the Amnesty Program.

**[08:08:26.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006915)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker.

**[08:08:28.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006916)  
This representation is now ready to answer questions from our colleagues.

**[08:08:34.430] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-006917)  
Salamat, Vice Chair Joboy. Majority Leader.

**[08:08:38.200] Rep. Katrina Reiko Chua-Tai** (asr-006918)  
Mr. Speaker, I move that we recognize

**[08:08:40.240] Rep. Katrina Reiko Chua-Tai** (asr-006919)  
the Honorable Presley C. De Jesus from PHILRECA Party-list

**[08:08:45.240] Rep. Katrina Reiko Chua-Tai** (asr-006920)  
to interpellate the proposed budget of the National Amnesty Commission.

**[08:08:50.240] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-006921)  
The gentleman from

**[08:08:52.240] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-006922)  
PHILRECA Party-list,

**[08:08:54.240] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-006923)  
Honorable Presley De Jesus.

**[08:08:58.240] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-006924)  
Sir, you may interpellate the sponsor.

**[08:09:03.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006925)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker.

**[08:09:05.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006926)  
I have only a few questions.

**[08:09:07.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006927)  
First, the National Amnesty Commission was created precisely

**[08:09:12.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006928)  
to receive, process, and determine whether applicants are entitled to amnesty

**[08:09:18.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006929)  
under Executive Order No. 125,

**[08:09:20.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006930)  
as amended by Executive Order 47.

**[08:09:23.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006931)  
This is the Commission's principal statutory mandate.

**[08:09:28.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006932)  
The application period for the four amnesty proclamations

**[08:09:32.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006933)  
ran from March 2024 until March 2026.

**[08:09:37.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006934)  
Yet, based on the Commission's reported figures,

**[08:09:41.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006935)  
only 20 applicants have been granted amnesty

**[08:09:45.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006936)  
by the close of the application period,

**[08:09:47.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006937)  
out of approximately 16,000 applicants.

**[08:09:49.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006938)  
This is equivalent to only 0.1232% of applications received.

**[08:10:01.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006939)  
How does the Commission justify this level of output

**[08:10:05.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006940)  
after two full years of implementation?

**[08:10:09.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006941)  
More importantly, how many of the 16,230 applications

**[08:10:14.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006942)  
have actually been fully evaluated and resolved?

**[08:10:17.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006943)  
Whether approved, denied, or otherwise disposed of?

**[08:10:22.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006944)  
And how many remain pending?

**[08:10:31.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006945)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker.

**[08:10:38.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006946)  
There were several factors that affect this and reasons.

**[08:10:44.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006947)  
Number one, the incomplete submitted documents.

**[08:10:46.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006948)  
Many applications lack documents necessary

**[08:10:51.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006949)  
for the complete review of the amnesty application.

**[08:10:54.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006950)  
Requiring them to submit lacking documents

**[08:10:58.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006951)  
is challenging due to logistical and financial concerns,

**[08:11:03.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006952)  
as well as reluctance of other agencies to coordinate.

**[08:11:08.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006953)  
NAC response: continuous streamlining and simplification

**[08:11:14.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006954)  
of process, determining which documentary requirements

**[08:11:19.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006955)  
can be dispensed with.

**[08:11:21.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006956)  
Number two, unresponsive and uncooperative agencies.

**[08:11:26.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006957)  
Some agencies are not familiar with the NAC.

**[08:11:29.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006958)  
Some are reluctant to share documents with the NAC.

**[08:11:33.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006959)  
Some use the Data Privacy Act to refuse NAC access

**[08:11:39.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006960)  
to requested documents.

**[08:11:42.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006961)  
Continuous engagement with these agencies

**[08:11:47.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006962)  
to make NAC known to them.

**[08:11:50.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006963)  
Orientations on the mandate of the NAC,

**[08:11:53.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006964)  
reiteration that the request for documents

**[08:11:57.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006965)  
is pursuant to NAC's mandate,

**[08:12:00.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006966)  
making it official function.

**[08:12:04.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006967)  
Number three, difficulty in declaring a quorum

**[08:12:08.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006968)  
for lab members.

**[08:12:10.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006969)  
Many amnesty applications need to undergo

**[08:12:13.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006970)  
a case conference by lab members.

**[08:12:16.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006971)  
Each lab has five members,

**[08:12:18.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006972)  
which a quorum fixed at three members

**[08:12:21.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006973)  
to formally constitute a body.

**[08:12:24.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006974)  
These lab members are employed primarily

**[08:12:27.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006975)  
in different offices.

**[08:12:29.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006976)  
Thus, their schedules are usually difficult to manage,

**[08:12:33.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006977)  
resulting in regular meetings.

**[08:12:37.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006978)  
Ensuring honoraria are released

**[08:12:40.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006979)  
in a timely manner.

**[08:12:42.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006980)  
Proper coordination and engagement

**[08:12:45.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006981)  
with the lab members,

**[08:12:46.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006982)  
capacitating lab secretariat

**[08:12:49.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006983)  
on how to coordinate better with lab members.

**[08:12:53.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006984)  
Number four, different review process.

**[08:12:57.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006985)  
Review process by the ex-official members

**[08:13:01.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006986)  
involve a separate and independent process

**[08:13:05.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006987)  
and standard.

**[08:13:07.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006988)  
This takes up time.

**[08:13:09.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006989)  
It takes up time that is not within the process

**[08:13:13.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006990)  
that can be controlled by NAC.

**[08:13:17.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006991)  
NAC response is to continue coordination,

**[08:13:23.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006992)  
streamlining of process,

**[08:13:25.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006993)  
regular process check,

**[08:13:27.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006994)  
and technical working group meetings.

**[08:13:30.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006995)  
Designation of personnel

**[08:13:32.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006996)  
to specifically follow up

**[08:13:35.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006997)  
with each ex-official member.

**[08:13:39.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006998)  
Number five, low manpower.

**[08:13:43.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-006999)  
With the postponement of hiring of COS

**[08:13:47.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-007000)  
imposed by civil service,

**[08:13:50.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-007001)  
the NAC operates with its limited manpower.

**[08:13:57.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-007002)  
With the surge of the amnesty application

**[08:14:00.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-007003)  
received corresponding increase in manpower

**[08:14:03.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-007004)  
to who will process this amnesty application.

**[08:14:08.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-007005)  
Necessary.

**[08:14:10.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-007006)  
The response of NAC.

**[08:14:12.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-007007)  
Letter request sent to COA DBM

**[08:14:16.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-007008)  
and civil service for an exemption.

**[08:14:19.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-007009)  
Streamlining of process to simplify

**[08:14:22.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-007010)  
or shorten process for faster processing.

**[08:14:26.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-007011)  
Continuous capacity building

**[08:14:29.240] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-007012)  
and process checking.

**[09:09:24.480] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-008249)  
Susunod pong katanungan, sa ilalim naman din po ng special provisions ng 2027 NEP,

**[09:09:32.200] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-008250)  
ang OPA-PRO ay merong...

**[09:09:34.080] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-008251)  
ang obligasyon na i-validate ang implementation ng PAMANA program by the DSWD, NCIP, at PhilHealth.

**[09:09:43.680] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-008252)  
At ang kabuoang halaga na ini-implement ng mga ahensya na ito ay nasa 1.4 billion pesos.

**[09:09:52.080] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-008253)  
Mr. Sponsor, maaari bang ipaliwanag kung ano ang pagkakaiba nito sa 6.9 billion

**[09:10:00.100] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-008254)  
that will be directly implemented by the OPA-PRO?

**[09:10:03.560] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-008255)  
Yung nabanggit niyo po na yung mga NCIP, PhilHealth, DSWD, PhilHealth, and NCIP,

**[09:10:24.000] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-008256)  
diretso po ito sa agencies such as DSWD, PhilHealth, and NCIP.

**[09:10:29.560] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-008257)  
Pero ang OPA-PRO po ay may obligasyon.

**[09:10:33.520] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-008258)  
Na i-validate ito. Tama po ba?

**[09:10:46.720] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-008259)  
Yes, binabalidate po ng OPA-PRO.

**[09:10:50.000] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-008260)  
Oversight po ang OPA-PRO sa mga projects.

**[09:10:52.140] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-008261)  
At kung aking babalikan po kanina, ibig sabihin na yung 1.4 billion ay directang mapupunta sa mga ahensyang nasabi

**[09:11:01.920] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-008262)  
at ito'y nakaseparate doon sa 6.9 billion. Tama?

**[09:11:07.120] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-008263)  
Yes, diretso po sa kanila.

**[09:11:08.900] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-008264)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[09:11:10.320] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-008265)  
Mr. Speaker, Mr. Sponsor, mas malaki na ang ginagampanan role ng OPA-PRO sa pagpapatupad ng pamana program.

**[09:11:19.840] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-008266)  
Paano masisiguro ng OPA-PRO na meron itong capacity para i-implement ito effectively

**[09:11:26.660] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-008267)  
at ano ang 2027 targets ng OPA-PRO para sa pamana program?

**[09:11:31.920] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-008268)  
Ang OPA-PRO ay hindi po nag-implement.

**[09:11:41.400] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-008269)  
Nag-aano lang po ng download ng funds.

**[09:11:48.000] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-008270)  
It's either the local government or maybe DPWH.

**[09:11:53.460] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-008271)  
Ang agency po, hindi po ang OPA-PRO.

**[09:11:57.980] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-008272)  
Oversight lang po ang OPA-PRO.

**[09:12:00.200] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-008273)  
Opo.

**[09:12:01.920] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-008274)  
Kanina sa katanungan ko sa 1.4 billion.

**[09:12:06.520] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-008275)  
Itong pang-apat kong tanong po, paano po kaya masisiguro ng OPA-PRO na meron itong capacity para i-implement effectively

**[09:12:15.800] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-008276)  
at ano ang 2027 targets ng OPA-PRO para sa pamana program?

**[09:12:27.520] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-008277)  
We have specific nandito po.

**[09:12:31.920] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-008278)  
Targets for every region.

**[09:12:34.560] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-008279)  
Okay po.

**[09:12:38.580] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-008280)  
For every region.

**[09:12:40.680] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-008281)  
Sige po.

**[09:45:56.880] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-008705)
